                - list [ref=e289]:
                  - listitem [ref=e290]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e291]: To improve security, Palette now enforces new permission and credential checks on the registry APIs. Automation that calls the following endpoints must use a principal that holds the required permission. Otherwise, the request fails with an authorization error.
                    - table [ref=e292]:
                      - rowgroup [ref=e293]:

                          - cell "POST /v1/registries/helm/validate with a saved password or token" [ref=e316]:

                            - text: with a saved password or token

                          - cell "POST /v1/registries/oci/basic/validate with a saved password or token" [ref=e322]:
                            - code [ref=e323]: POST /v1/registries/oci/basic/validate
                            - text: with a saved password or token

                            - text: identifies
                    - paragraph [ref=e327]:
                      - text: Palette uses the saved password or token of a registry only with the saved endpoint of that registry. If you change the endpoint of a registry, re-enter its password or token. Otherwise, the validate and update requests fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                      - code [ref=e328]: RegistryMaskedCredentialEndpointMismatch
                      - text: error code.
                    - paragraph [ref=e329]:
                      - text: Palette also no longer validates a saved private Helm registry with its saved credentials, even for a principal with
                      - code [ref=e330]: packRegistry.update
                      - text: . The
                      - code [ref=e331]: POST /v1/registries/helm/validate
                      - text: request fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                      - code [ref=e332]: HelmRegistryValidationForbidden
                      - text: error code. Enter the credentials in the request instead.
                    - paragraph [ref=e333]: Registry API responses now return an empty string for a password, token, or secret key that was never set, instead of a masked value. A masked value indicates that the secret is set.
                - list [ref=e334]:
                  - listitem [ref=e335]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e336]:
                      - text: To improve security, Palette now validates the property names in the
                      - code [ref=e337]: filters
                      - text: and
                      - code [ref=e338]: orderBy
                      - text: query parameters of Palette API list endpoints. A request fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                      - code [ref=e339]: FilterValidationFailure
                      - text: "error code if a property name meets any of the following conditions:"
                    - list [ref=e340]:
                      - listitem [ref=e341]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e342]: It is empty or longer than 256 characters.
                      - listitem [ref=e343]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e344]:
                          - text: It contains characters other than letters, numbers, underscores (
                          - code [ref=e345]: _
                          - text: ), hyphens (
                          - code [ref=e346]: "-"
                          - text: ), forward slashes (
                          - code [ref=e347]: /
                          - text: ), and periods (
                          - code [ref=e348]: .
                          - text: ).
                      - listitem [ref=e349]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e350]:
                          - text: It contains an empty segment, such as
                          - code [ref=e351]: metadata..name
                          - text: ", or a segment that begins with a hyphen."
                      - listitem [ref=e352]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e353]: It references a protected field, such as a field that stores a password, key, or token, or a field nested under a protected field.
                    - paragraph [ref=e354]:
                      - text: Filters and sort keys on other properties, such as
                      - code [ref=e355]: metadata.name
                      - text: ", are unaffected. Update any automation that filters or sorts on a property that meets these conditions."
                - list [ref=e356]:
                  - listitem [ref=e357]:
                    - text: To improve security, system API responses now return stored secrets, such as passwords, access keys, and client secrets, as
                    - code [ref=e358]: "********"
                    - text: . A secret that was never set still returns an empty string. Automation that reads a secret from the system APIs must take it from its source instead, such as the identity provider, cloud storage account, or registry. To keep a stored secret when you update a system configuration, send
                    - code [ref=e359]: "********"
                    - text: in that field of the
                    - code [ref=e360]: PUT
                    - text: request.
                - list [ref=e361]:
                  - listitem [ref=e362]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e363]: To improve security, Palette now restricts the following user and tenant API requests. Update any automation that sends them.
                    - list [ref=e364]:
                      - listitem [ref=e365]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e366]:
                          - code [ref=e367]: GET /v1/users
                          - text: and
                          - code [ref=e368]: GET /v1/tenants
                          - text: no longer accept
                          - code [ref=e369]: status.login.mode
                          - text: or
                          - code [ref=e370]: status.isActive
                          - text: in the
                          - code [ref=e371]: filters
                          - text: or
                          - code [ref=e372]: orderBy
                          - text: query parameters. Requests that use these fields fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                          - code [ref=e373]: FilterValidationFailure
                          - text: error code.
                      - listitem [ref=e374]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e375]:
                          - code [ref=e376]: "PATCH /v1/users/{uid}"
                          - text: accepts only the
                          - code [ref=e377]: add
                          - text: ","
                          - code [ref=e378]: replace
                          - text: ","
                          - code [ref=e379]: remove
                          - text: ", and"
                          - code [ref=e380]: test
                          - text: operations on the
                          - code [ref=e381]: /spec/firstName
                          - text: ","
                          - code [ref=e382]: /spec/lastName
                          - text: ","
                          - code [ref=e383]: /spec/emailId
                          - text: ", and"
                          - code [ref=e384]: /spec/roles
                          - text: paths. Requests to patch other paths fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                          - code [ref=e385]: UserPatchPathNotAllowed
                          - text: error code. The
                          - code [ref=e386]: move
                          - text: and
                          - code [ref=e387]: copy
                          - text: operations fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                          - code [ref=e388]: UserPatchOpNotAllowed
                          - text: error code.
                      - listitem [ref=e389]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e390]:
                          - text: On self-hosted Palette and Palette VerteX,
                          - code [ref=e391]: "GET /v1/users/{uid}/activationlink"
                          - text: now requires the
                          - code [ref=e392]: user.create
                          - text: permission to read another user's activation link. Requests from users without this permission fail with an HTTP 403 response. Reading your own activation link is unchanged. To read other users' activation links, use an account that has the
                          - code [ref=e393]: user.create
                          - text: permission.
                - list [ref=e394]:
                  - listitem [ref=e395]:
                    - text: Palette now rejects
                    - code [ref=e396]: "PUT /v1/clusterTemplates/{uid}/profiles"
                    - text: requests whose scope does not match the scope of the cluster template. For example, a request with a
                    - code [ref=e397]: ProjectUid
                    - text: header targeting a tenant-scoped cluster template returns an HTTP 403 response and the
                    - code [ref=e398]: ResOperationForbidden
                    - text: error code. Omit the
                    - code [ref=e399]: ProjectUid
                    - text: header for a tenant-scoped template, and set it to the UID of the project that owns a project-scoped template. Refer to
                    - link "Modify Cluster Templates" [ref=e400] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /cluster-templates/modify-cluster-templates/#prerequisites
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e401]:
                  - listitem [ref=e402]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e403]:
                      - text: Palette now rejects Kubernetes upgrades that skip a minor version or change the major version. Upgrade one minor version at a time, as the
                      - link "Kubernetes version skew policy" [ref=e404] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/releases/version-skew-policy/#supported-component-upgrade-order
                      - text: "requires, for example from 1.33 to 1.34 and then to 1.35. The check applies to the following changes:"
                    - list [ref=e405]:
                      - listitem [ref=e406]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e407]: Cluster updates, including pending and scheduled cluster template updates.
                      - listitem [ref=e408]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e409]: Updates to a cluster profile that clusters use. The profile cannot move more than one minor version ahead of the oldest Kubernetes version running on those clusters.
                      - listitem [ref=e410]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e411]: Cluster profile changes in a cluster template, and attaching a cluster to a cluster template.
                    - paragraph [ref=e412]:
                      - text: Rejected requests from the Palette UI, API, Terraform, and Crossplane fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                      - code [ref=e413]: K8sUpgradeNotSupported
                      - text: error code, and the cluster stays on its current version. Patch upgrades and imported clusters are not affected. Refer to
                      - link "Update a Cluster" [ref=e414] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cluster-updates/#limitations
                      - text: for more information.
                - heading "Upgrade NotesDirect link to Upgrade Notes" [level=4] [ref=e415]:
                  - text: Upgrade Notes
                  - link "Direct link to Upgrade Notes" [ref=e416] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#upgrade-notes-4.10.b"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e417]:
                  - listitem [ref=e418]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e419]:
                      - strong [ref=e420]: "User action required:"
                      - text: The AKS-managed CSI drivers on AKS clusters deployed with a service principal cloud account now use the
                      - link "AKS control plane identity" [ref=e421] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/aks/managed-identity-overview#summary-of-managed-identities-used-by-aks
                      - text: instead of the cloud account's service principal. This applies to the Azure Files, Azure Disk, and Azure Blob CSI drivers. If these clusters use storage outside the AKS node resource group, grant the AKS control plane identity access to that storage. This includes StorageClasses with a custom
                      - code [ref=e422]: resourceGroup
                      - text: parameter, static persistent volumes in other resource groups, and disk encryption sets. Otherwise, provisioning or attaching these volumes fails with HTTP 403 errors. On self-hosted Palette and Palette VerteX, grant this access before you upgrade.
                    - paragraph [ref=e423]:
                      - text: After the upgrade, Palette restarts the enabled CSI drivers on each of these clusters, which takes a few minutes per cluster. Pods that start or move during the restart wait until the drivers are back, and running pods keep their volumes. If Palette cannot restart the drivers, it raises a warning event on the cluster. Refer to
                      - link "AKS CSI Drivers Fail to Restart After Palette Upgrade" [ref=e424] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /troubleshooting/cluster-deployment/#scenario---aks-csi-drivers-fail-to-restart-after-palette-upgrade
                      - text: for more information.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e425]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e426] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e427]:
                  - listitem [ref=e428]:
                    - text: Users who sign in to a Palette tenant with a password can now add a passkey as a second authentication factor from their
                    - strong [ref=e429]: My Profile
                    - text: ">"
                    - strong [ref=e430]: Security
                    - text: tab. After a user adds a passkey, Palette asks for it each time the user signs in with a password, in any tenant the user belongs to. Tenant administrators can also require every user who signs in to the tenant with a password to add a passkey within a configurable grace period (14 days by default). Palette does not ask for a passkey for SSO sign-ins or for API requests authenticated with API keys. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Manage Passkeys" [ref=e431] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /user-management/authentication/passkeys/
                    - text: and
                    - link "Passkey Enforcement" [ref=e432] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /tenant-settings/passkeys/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e433]:
                  - listitem [ref=e434]:
                    - text: You can now deploy the Headlamp dashboard to Palette Virtual Clusters that use the Kubernetes distribution, in
                    - strong [ref=e435]: Proxied
                    - text: or
                    - strong [ref=e436]: Direct
                    - text: access mode. Headlamp authenticates through the OIDC configuration of the virtual cluster or its host cluster, or through Palette OIDC if neither has one. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Headlamp" [ref=e437] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/headlamp/#virtual-clusters
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e438]:
                  - listitem [ref=e439]:
                    - text: Palette now provides system macros that resolve to the OIDC configuration of a cluster, including the issuer URL, client ID, client secret, username and groups claims, and extra scopes. Reference them in pack values, for example
                    - code [ref=e440]: "{{.spectro.system.oidc.issuerurl}}"
                    - text: ", to place OIDC settings in the keys and format that your Kubernetes distribution requires. For more information, refer to"
                    - link "OIDC Macros" [ref=e441] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/pack-constraints/#oidc-macros
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e442]:
                  - listitem [ref=e443]:
                    - text: You can now authenticate Azure cloud accounts with a user-assigned managed identity instead of a service principal and client secret. Managed identity cloud accounts store no secret and work through a self-hosted Private Cloud Gateway (PCG) deployed in Azure. You can also assign managed identities to Azure IaaS and AKS clusters, so that the clusters access Azure without static credentials. The
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e444] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://registry.terraform.io/providers/spectrocloud/spectrocloud/latest/docs
                    - text: also supports managed identity cloud accounts. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Use Azure Managed Identities" [ref=e445] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/azure/azure-managed-identities/
                    - text: .
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e446]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e447] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e448]:
                  - listitem [ref=e449]:
                    - text: When you add a GCP backup location, you can now upload the JSON key file of the service account instead of pasting its contents. This matches how you add a GCP cloud account. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Add a Backup Location using Static Credentials" [ref=e450] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/backup-restore/add-backup-location-static/#add-a-gcp-bucket
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e451]:
                  - listitem [ref=e452]:
                    - text: Palette now records an audit log entry each time someone downloads a kubeconfig file for a cluster or virtual cluster from the Palette UI or the API, including failed and denied attempts. Each entry identifies the user, the cluster, and the kubeconfig file. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Kubeconfig Downloads" [ref=e453] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /audit-logs/#kubeconfig-downloads
                    - text: .
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e454]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e455] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e456]:
                  - listitem [ref=e457]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused
                    - link "cluster cost" [ref=e458] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cloud-cost/
                    - text: ", resource usage, and workload summary data to stop updating for clusters whose data grew past the 16 MB MongoDB document size limit."
                - list [ref=e459]:
                  - listitem [ref=e460]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Palette to add an empty
                    - code [ref=e461]: auth
                    - text: value,
                    - code [ref=e462]: Og==
                    - text: ", to the containerd configuration of self-hosted Palette and Palette VerteX nodes when the"
                    - link "custom image registry" [ref=e463] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/system-management/registry-override/
                    - text: was configured with an empty username and password. Palette now leaves out the authentication configuration when both values are empty.
                - list [ref=e464]:
                  - listitem [ref=e465]: Fixed an issue that stopped cluster creation before any worker nodes were provisioned. This occurred when CNI pack pods that cannot run on control plane nodes, such as Cilium Hubble Relay and Hubble UI, were still pending 30 minutes after the CNI pack was installed. Pending CNI pack pods no longer block worker node creation.
                - list [ref=e466]:
                  - listitem [ref=e467]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that left the deprecated
                    - code [ref=e468]: capa-eks-control-plane-controller-manager
                    - text: Deployment in the cluster namespace of
                    - link "EKS clusters" [ref=e469] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: without add-on packs. The Deployment's pod restarted repeatedly. Palette now removes the Deployment and its related namespace-scoped resources.
                - list [ref=e470]:
                  - listitem [ref=e471]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused a pack to stay in progress indefinitely when its Deployment rollout failed with
                    - code [ref=e472]: ProgressDeadlineExceeded
                    - text: ", for example, after a cluster profile update with incorrect pack values. Palette now reports the pack as failed and shows the error message from the Deployment."
                - list [ref=e473]:
                  - listitem [ref=e474]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Palette and Cluster API controller pods to keep running on workload node pools after you
                    - link "dedicated a node pool for system pods" [ref=e475] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#dedicated-system-pod-pool
                    - text: on an existing EKS cluster. Palette now labels the nodes in the dedicated node pool and schedules these controllers on them.
                - list [ref=e476]:
                  - listitem [ref=e477]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the Palette agent on a workload cluster to delete and recreate the namespaces that Palette created through
                    - link "cluster Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) settings" [ref=e478] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/namespace-management/
                    - text: or a workspace. This occurred when the Palette management plane briefly lost access to its database, and the recreated namespaces were empty.
                - list [ref=e479]:
                  - listitem [ref=e480]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented the cluster autoscaler pod from being scheduled on control plane nodes. When all worker
                    - link "node pools" [ref=e481] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/
                    - text: carried a
                    - code [ref=e482]: NoSchedule
                    - text: taint, the pod stayed
                    - code [ref=e483]: Pending
                    - text: after a node drain, repave, or upgrade, and node pool autoscaling stopped.
                - list [ref=e484]:
                  - listitem [ref=e485]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the AKS-managed CSI drivers on AKS clusters deployed with a service principal cloud account to use the cloud account's service principal instead of the AKS control plane identity. Dynamic volume provisioning failed with HTTP 403 errors when only the AKS control plane identity had storage permissions. Palette no longer creates the
                    - code [ref=e486]: azure-cloud-provider
                    - text: secret in the
                    - code [ref=e487]: kube-system
                    - text: namespace of AKS clusters. After the upgrade, Palette removes the secret that it created and restarts the drivers, and persistent volume claims stuck in
                    - code [ref=e488]: Pending
                    - text: then bind automatically. Refer to the
                    - link "Upgrade Notes" [ref=e489] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: "#upgrade-notes-4.10.b"
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e490]:
                  - listitem [ref=e491]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused repeated
                    - code [ref=e492]: Operation 'cloudconfig.get' is forbidden
                    - text: errors after you switched projects while a cluster's details page was still loading. The errors appeared even though your role had the required permissions.
                - list [ref=e493]:
                  - listitem [ref=e494]:
                    - text: Fixed issues that caused clusters attached to a
                    - link "cluster template" [ref=e495] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /cluster-templates/modify-cluster-templates/#update-cluster-profile-version
                    - text: to revert to the default values of their profile variables after the template's cluster profile version changed. The issues occurred when you selected
                    - strong [ref=e496]: Review & Save
                    - text: on a tenant-scoped cluster template from a project, or when a Palette API or Terraform request did not list every profile variable.
                - list [ref=e497]:
                  - listitem [ref=e498]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the Palette UI to reject valid
                    - link "node pool" [ref=e499] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/
                    - strong [ref=e500]: Additional Labels
                    - text: whose value is a single character, such as
                    - code [ref=e501]: workload:a
                    - text: ", and to highlight the wrong label."
                - list [ref=e502]:
                  - listitem [ref=e503]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Headlamp to stay on its loading screen when you opened it from a cluster's
                    - strong [ref=e504]: Connect
                    - text: link through Spectro Proxy. This occurred when Palette took longer than 15 seconds to respond, for example, over a slow network connection.
                - list [ref=e505]:
                  - listitem [ref=e506]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused cluster profile creation through the Palette API or Terraform to fail with
                    - code [ref=e507]: "ErrPackUidInvalid: Pack uid '<uid>' not found"
                    - text: ", even though the requested pack version existed. This occurred when the request specified a pack by registry, name, and version without a pack UID."
                - list [ref=e508]:
                  - listitem [ref=e509]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused clusters to remain marked as cluster group hosts after their
                    - link "cluster group" [ref=e510] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-groups/
                    - text: was deleted. Palette now clears this state when a cluster group is deleted. Upgrading Palette also clears it from clusters that are already affected.
                - list [ref=e511]:
                  - listitem [ref=e512]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused clusters created from the Palette UI to have the
                    - strong [ref=e513]: Update worker pools in parallel
                    - text: option (
                    - code [ref=e514]: updateWorkerPoolsInParallel
                    - text: ) turned on, while clusters created through the Palette API or Terraform had it turned off. Clusters created from the Palette UI now have this option turned off by default.
                - heading "Deprecations and RemovalsDirect link to Deprecations and Removals" [level=4] [ref=e515]:
                  - text: Deprecations and Removals
                  - link "Direct link to Deprecations and Removals" [ref=e516] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#deprecations-and-removals"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e517]:
                  - listitem [ref=e518]:
                    - text: Palette removes the deprecated
                    - code [ref=e519]: GET /v1/clusterprofiles
                    - text: endpoint. Use
                    - code [ref=e520]: POST /v1/dashboard/clusterprofiles
                    - text: to list cluster profiles instead. Other
                    - code [ref=e521]: /v1/clusterprofiles
                    - text: endpoints, such as those that create, update, and publish cluster profiles, continue to work. Update any automation, script, or integration that calls
                    - code [ref=e522]: GET /v1/clusterprofiles
                    - text: before you upgrade, because those calls fail after the upgrade.
                - list [ref=e523]:
                  - listitem [ref=e524]:
                    - text: The
                    - code [ref=e525]: POST /v1/users/kubectl/session
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e526]: "GET /v1/users/kubectl/session/{sessionUid}"
                    - text: ", and"
                    - code [ref=e527]: "DELETE /v1/users/kubectl/session/{sessionUid}"
                    - text: Palette
                    - link "API endpoints" [ref=e528] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /api/introduction/
                    - text: are now deprecated. These endpoints supported web-based
                    - code [ref=e529]: kubectl
                    - text: ", which is no longer available in Palette, so they have no replacement. Remove any calls to these endpoints from your automation, scripts, and integrations. Refer to the"
                    - link "Deprecations" [ref=e530] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /release-notes/announcements/#deprecations
                    - text: table for the removal timeline.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e531]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e532] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e533]:
                  - generic [ref=e534]:
                    - img [ref=e536]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e539]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e540] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.44 Palette release is 4.10.14.
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=4] [ref=e541]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e542] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-edge-4.10.b"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e543]:
                  - listitem [ref=e544]:
                    - text: Local UI now rejects cluster updates that move Kubernetes more than one minor version ahead of the version last applied to the cluster or that downgrade Kubernetes after a successful upgrade. Local UI displays an error, and the cluster stays on its current configuration. To upgrade by more than one minor version, upload a cluster profile version for each minor version in turn. Requests to the Edge Management API fail with an HTTP 400 response and the
                    - code [ref=e545]: K8sMultiMinorUpgradeNotSupported
                    - text: or
                    - code [ref=e546]: K8sDowngradeAfterUpgradeNotSupported
                    - text: error code. Refer to
                    - link "Update Local Cluster" [ref=e547] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/cluster-management/update-cluster/#kubernetes-version-limits
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "Upgrade NotesDirect link to Upgrade Notes" [level=4] [ref=e548]:
                  - text: Upgrade Notes
                  - link "Direct link to Upgrade Notes" [ref=e549] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#upgrade-notes-edge-4.10.b"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e550]:
                  - listitem [ref=e551]:
                    - text: After you upgrade the Palette agent on an existing Edge cluster, Palette manages its default Cert Manager installation with Helm so that you can replace it with the
                    - link "Cert Manager" [ref=e552] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cert-manager-addon/
                    - text: pack. The Cert Manager pods might restart once during the change. Certificates that Cert Manager has already issued remain valid.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e553]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e554] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e555]:
                  - listitem [ref=e556]:
                    - text: You can now add the Cert Manager pack to the cluster profiles of Edge clusters to customize Cert Manager, either when you deploy the cluster or later. The pack takes over the default Cert Manager installation from Palette, and Palette restores its default configuration if you remove the pack. Refer to
                    - link "Cert Manager Add-On" [ref=e557] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cert-manager-addon/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e558]:
                  - listitem [ref=e559]:
                    - text: Edge clusters can now authenticate to a private Amazon Elastic Container Registry (ECR) repository without storing static AWS credentials on the Edge hosts. Palette issues short-lived ECR tokens that the Edge hosts use to pull provider images during cluster creation and upgrades. This feature is available only for centrally managed clusters in appliance mode. Refer to
                    - link "Deploy Cluster with an Amazon ECR Provider Registry" [ref=e560] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/deploy-custom-registries/deploy-ecr-provider-registry/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e561]:
                  - listitem [ref=e562]:
                    - text: Centrally managed Edge Native clusters now support an update lock that holds cluster profile updates on the Edge hosts until an operator releases them in Local UI or through the Edge Management API. Each release lets one update through, and then the lock engages again. Turn on
                    - strong [ref=e563]: Lock Profile Updates
                    - text: when you create the cluster or later in the cluster settings. Refer to
                    - link "Lock Profile Updates" [ref=e564] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/lock-profile-updates/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e565]:
                  - listitem [ref=e566]:
                    - text: Local UI now lists the Local UI server certificate, the per-node mutual TLS (mTLS) certificates, and the registry certificates alongside the Kubernetes certificates of a cluster. For each certificate, Local UI shows the expiration date and whether Palette Edge renews it automatically. Local UI also warns you when certificates are close to their expiration date or have expired. Refer to
                    - link "View Cluster Certificates" [ref=e567] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/certificate-renewal/#view-cluster-certificates
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e568]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e569] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e570]:
                  - listitem [ref=e571]:
                    - text: You can now change how long each OS upgrade attempt can run on an Edge host with the
                    - code [ref=e572]: system.upgradeTimeoutMinutes
                    - text: parameter in the OS layer of the cluster profile. The default limit is now 45 minutes, up from 15 minutes. Increase the limit for Edge hosts with slow network connections. Refer to
                    - link "Configure the OS Upgrade Timeout" [ref=e573] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/os-upgrade-timeout/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e574]:
                  - listitem [ref=e575]:
                    - text: Local UI now displays an
                    - strong [ref=e576]: OS upgrade failed
                    - text: banner when an Edge host in appliance mode rolls back to its previous provider image after an OS upgrade fails. The banner appears on the
                    - strong [ref=e577]: Edge Host
                    - text: page and on the
                    - strong [ref=e578]: Overview
                    - text: tab of the
                    - strong [ref=e579]: Cluster
                    - text: page for both centrally managed and locally managed clusters. It stays until a later OS upgrade succeeds on the Edge host. Refer to
                    - link "OS Upgrade Fails and Edge Host Rolls Back to Previous Image" [ref=e580] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---os-upgrade-fails-and-edge-host-rolls-back-to-previous-image
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e581]:
                  - listitem [ref=e582]:
                    - text: When you sort the Edge host grid view by a column, Palette now orders all Edge hosts, so the order continues across pages instead of restarting on each page. This applies to the
                    - strong [ref=e583]: Edge Hosts
                    - text: tab and to the grid view that appears when you create an Edge cluster or add Edge hosts to an existing cluster. Refer to
                    - link "Edge Host Grid View" [ref=e584] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/edge-host-view/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e585]:
                  - listitem [ref=e586]:
                    - text: Palette and Local UI now display a warning when you create an Edge cluster without an overlay network, because IP address changes in a Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCP) network can cause cluster instability. Refer to
                    - link "Enable Overlay Network" [ref=e587] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/networking/vxlan-overlay/#when-should-you-consider-enabling-overlay-network
                    - text: for guidance on when to enable an overlay network.
                - list [ref=e588]:
                  - listitem [ref=e589]:
                    - text: The mTLS certificates that Edge hosts use to secure communication with each other are now valid for 10 years instead of 5 years. Refer to
                    - link "Renew Certificates for Local Clusters" [ref=e590] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/certificate-renewal/
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e591]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e592] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e593]:
                  - listitem [ref=e594]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused provider image downloads on Edge hosts with limited network bandwidth to fail repeatedly. The Palette agent now stops a download attempt only when it receives no data for 5 minutes, and the next attempt resumes the download. During a cluster update that changes the provider image, each download attempt still ends after 15 minutes. To resume a download, the Edge host needs free disk space of about three times the compressed size of the provider image. Palette also displays Edge host events during cluster provisioning when a provider image download starts, resumes, retries, or completes. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Troubleshooting - Edge" [ref=e595] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---provider-image-download-times-out-on-slow-network-connections
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e596]:
                  - listitem [ref=e597]: Fixed an issue that caused the Palette agent to reset an Edge host removed from a node pool without draining it first, so Kubernetes did not enforce PodDisruptionBudgets. The Palette agent now drains each removed host before it resets the host. If a PodDisruptionBudget blocks the drain, the host stays cordoned in the cluster until the drain can complete, for example, after you move the blocking workload to another node.
                - list [ref=e598]:
                  - listitem [ref=e599]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - link "Palette Edge Interactive Installer" [ref=e600] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edgeforge-workflow/palette-canvos/arg/
                    - text: to offer to continue after the installation media, such as a virtual CD or USB drive, was disconnected. The disk wipe failed with
                    - code [ref=e601]: "fork/exec /usr/sbin/wipefs: input/output error"
                    - text: ", and the installation later failed with a"
                    - code [ref=e602]: mount failed
                    - text: error. The installer now stops the installation and prompts you to reconnect the installation media and reboot the host.
                - list [ref=e603]:
                  - listitem [ref=e604]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented a control plane node from joining an Edge cluster that uses Palette eXtended Kubernetes - Edge (PXK-E) after a join attempt failed. The node kept the cluster's virtual IP (VIP) address from the failed attempt, so each retry failed with
                    - code [ref=e605]: "connect: connection refused"
                    - text: errors for the VIP. This was most likely to occur when two control plane nodes joined the cluster at the same time. A control plane node no longer keeps the VIP after a failed join attempt.
                - list [ref=e606]:
                  - listitem [ref=e607]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Local UI to display only the message
                    - code [ref=e608]: Client sent an HTTP request to an HTTPS server.
                    - text: when you opened it with an
                    - code [ref=e609]: http://
                    - text: address. Local UI now redirects HTTP requests on its port to HTTPS. Refer to
                    - link "Access Local UI" [ref=e610] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/host-management/access-console/#log-in-to-local-ui
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e611]:
                  - listitem [ref=e612]:
                    - text: Fixed issues that allowed a
                    - link "Local UI" [ref=e613] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/
                    - text: session to continue after it expired because of inactivity. Local UI sessions now end after the idle time set in
                    - code [ref=e614]: stylus.localUI.session.inactivityTimeoutInMinutes
                    - text: plus a 2-minute grace period. The
                    - code [ref=e615]: stylus.localUI.session.refreshTimeoutInMinutes
                    - text: parameter now sets the maximum session length from login. Each token renewal now returns a new refresh token, and each refresh token expires after the same idle time and grace period. Edge Management API clients must use the refresh token from the latest renewal response. Refer to
                    - link "Edge Installer Configuration Reference" [ref=e616] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edge-configuration/installer-reference/#palette-agent-parameters
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e617]:
                  - listitem [ref=e618]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused containerized workloads that use
                    - code [ref=e619]: sudo
                    - text: or PAM to fail on Edge hosts built from Ubuntu 24.04
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e620] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edgeforge-workflow/palette-canvos/
                    - text: images with a hardened
                    - code [ref=e621]: /etc/shadow
                    - text: file, for example, one with file mode
                    - code [ref=e622]: "0000"
                    - text: . The workloads failed with
                    - code [ref=e623]: "sudo: PAM account management error: Authentication service cannot retrieve authentication info"
                    - text: . Edge artifacts built with CanvOS version 4.10.14 or later include the fix.
                - list [ref=e624]:
                  - listitem [ref=e625]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused
                    - link "audit log" [ref=e626] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /audit-logs/
                    - text: entries for cloud configuration updates on Edge clusters, such as NTP, VIP, or SSH key changes, to show the cluster UID instead of the cluster name in the
                    - strong [ref=e627]: Resource Name
                    - text: field.

                    - text: is now available. For more details, refer to the Terraform provider

                    - text: .

                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e656]:
                  - listitem [ref=e657]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue in the
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e658] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /automation/terraform/
                    - text: that left an empty
                    - code [ref=e659]: cluster_profile
                    - text: entry in the Terraform state of cluster resources, such as
                    - code [ref=e660]: spectrocloud_cluster_eks
                    - text: ", after an apply that changed a"
                    - code [ref=e661]: cluster_profile
                    - text: block with both
                    - code [ref=e662]: pack
                    - text: overrides and
                    - code [ref=e663]: variables
                    - text: . As a result, later
                    - code [ref=e664]: terraform plan
                    - text: runs reported a cluster profile change when nothing had changed.

                - heading "November 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.34-rev.1Direct link to November 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.34-rev.1" [level=2] [ref=e876]:
                  - text: November 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.34-rev.1
                  - link "Direct link to November 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.34-rev.1" [ref=e877] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#november-15-2026---release-41034-rev1"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e878]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e879] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-3"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e880]:
                  - listitem [ref=e881]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Edge hosts to lose their connection to Palette after registration and appear as
                    - strong [ref=e882]: Unhealthy
                    - text: . The issue affected hosts that use
                    - link "Trusted Boot" [ref=e883] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/trusted-boot/
                    - text: and were installed from an Edge installer ISO with a Palette agent version earlier than 4.10. Upgrading Palette does not recover Edge hosts that are already affected. For the recovery steps, refer to
                    - link "Troubleshooting - Edge" [ref=e884] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---palette-agent-restarts-repeatedly-after-registration-on-trusted-boot-edge-hosts
                    - text: .

                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.34-rev.1 Palette release is 4.10.11-rev.1.

                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e929]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e930] [cursor=pointer]:

                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e931]:
                  - listitem [ref=e932]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue in the Spectro Cloud Terraform and Crossplane providers that caused Day-2
                    - code [ref=e933]: cloud_config
                    - text: updates to the
                    - code [ref=e934]: spectrocloud_cluster_edge_native
                    - text: resource to fail when
                    - code [ref=e935]: ssh_keys
                    - text: was not set.

                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1104]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1105] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-4"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1106]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1107]:
                    - img "Technical preview feature badge" [ref=e1108]
                    - text: Artifact Studio v2 is available as a Tech Preview. It adds self-service accounts, organization entitlements, and the Artifact Studio API. Your organization's entitlements determine which appliances, packs, and profiles you can download, and the API's
                    - code [ref=e1109]: /v2/
                    - text: endpoints support pagination. Artifact Studio v1 is the default. Refer to
                    - link "Artifact Studio v2" [ref=e1110] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/artifact-studio-v2/
                    - text: and the
                    - link "Artifact Studio API" [ref=e1111] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /api/introduction/#artifact-studio-api
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e1112]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1113]:
                    - text: Added Kubernetes 1.37 to the FIPS and non-FIPS variants of the
                    - code [ref=e1114]: kubernetes-eks
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e1115]: kubernetes-aks
                    - text: packs for Amazon EKS and Azure AKS clusters.

                - heading "October 29, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.25Direct link to October 29, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.25" [level=2] [ref=e1247]:
                  - text: October 29, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.25
                  - link "Direct link to October 29, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.25" [ref=e1248] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vm-launchpad-4.10.25"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e1249]: This release delivers version 4.10.25 of the PaletteAI VM Launchpad appliance, which runs on Palette 4.10.34.
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=3] [ref=e1250]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e1251] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-vm-launchpad-4.10.25"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1252]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1253]:
                    - text: If you turned on
                    - strong [ref=e1254]: Restrict Allowed VLANs
                    - text: (
                    - code [ref=e1255]: vlanFiltering.enabled
                    - text: ) in an earlier release, review its settings before you upgrade. The
                    - strong [ref=e1256]: VM VLAN Range
                    - text: (
                    - code [ref=e1257]: vlanFiltering.env.allowedVlans
                    - text: ) replaces the
                    - code [ref=e1258]: vlanFiltering.env.vlanRange
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e1259]: vlanFiltering.env.enforceVlanRange
                    - text: settings, and VMO ignores the old values without a warning. After the upgrade, the VLAN filtering agent removes VLANs that no Network Attachment Definition (NAD) references. It also removes NAD VLANs that are outside the
                    - strong [ref=e1260]: VM VLAN Range
                    - text: ", including VLANs that NAD discovery added in an earlier release. Add every VLAN that your virtual machine networks use to the"
                    - strong [ref=e1261]: VM VLAN Range
                    - text: before you upgrade. The
                    - code [ref=e1262]: vlanFiltering.env.nadDiscovery
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e1263]: vlanFiltering.env.vlanDesiredStateSync
                    - text: parameters are deprecated. Refer to
                    - link "Upgrade Considerations" [ref=e1264] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/infrastructure/networking/#upgrade-considerations
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1265]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1266]:
                    - text: Nodes on non-FIPS appliances now reserve memory for host services, and etcd and the Kubernetes API server now request memory on each control plane node of every appliance. This prevents a large virtual machine from taking the memory that the control plane needs. On non-FIPS appliances, the upgrade lowers the allocatable memory of each node by about 4 GiB and its allocatable ephemeral storage by 10 GiB. A node that is already near its limit can reject or evict pods when the Kubelet restarts during the upgrade. Before you upgrade, confirm that each node has room for the reservation and the new memory requests. Refer to
                    - link "Check Node Memory Before You Upgrade" [ref=e1267] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/vmo-overcommit-memory-optimization-appliance/#check-node-memory-before-you-upgrade
                    - text: .
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=3] [ref=e1268]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e1269] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-4"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1270]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1271]:
                    - text: PaletteAI VM Launchpad now supports VLAN filtering on Linux Bridge networks. Virtual machines receive only the VLANs that their NADs assign, and only when the
                    - strong [ref=e1272]: VM VLAN Range
                    - text: permits them. The appliance protects the VLANs that the nodes use for their own traffic, and the
                    - strong [ref=e1273]: Networks
                    - text: page shows a
                    - strong [ref=e1274]: VLAN filtering
                    - text: panel when a node has a problem. Refer to
                    - link "Bridge VLAN Filtering" [ref=e1275] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/infrastructure/networking/#bridge-vlan-filtering
                    - text: .
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1276]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1277] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-5"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1278]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1279]:
                    - text: VMO now uses KubeVirt 1.9.0, which turns on its Beta features by default. VMO disables the Beta features that it has not validated, on both new installations and upgrades, and lists them in the new
                    - strong [ref=e1280]: Disabled feature gates
                    - text: field on the
                    - strong [ref=e1281]: KubeVirt Configuration
                    - text: page. KubeVirt 1.9.0 also deprecates the
                    - code [ref=e1282]: HotplugVolumes
                    - text: feature gate in favor of
                    - code [ref=e1283]: DeclarativeHotplugVolumes
                    - text: ", which is on by default. VMO still includes"
                    - code [ref=e1284]: HotplugVolumes
                    - text: in
                    - strong [ref=e1285]: Feature gates
                    - text: by default on both new installations and upgrades, so remove it from
                    - strong [ref=e1286]: Feature gates
                    - text: . Disk hot-plug keeps working as long as
                    - code [ref=e1287]: DeclarativeHotplugVolumes
                    - text: stays on. Refer to
                    - link "Feature Gate Considerations" [ref=e1288] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/kubevirt-configuration/#feature-gate-considerations
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1289]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1290]: VMO now uses Containerized Data Importer (CDI) 1.66.1, which fixes importing a disk image to a DataVolume on a block device with a 4K sector size.
                - list [ref=e1291]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1292]:
                    - text: The
                    - strong [ref=e1293]: Forwarding Token
                    - text: in the
                    - strong [ref=e1294]: Logs
                    - text: section of the
                    - strong [ref=e1295]: Metrics and Logs
                    - text: page now accepts a single
                    - code [ref=e1296]: Key=Value
                    - text: header, so you can forward logs to Datadog (
                    - code [ref=e1297]: DD-API-KEY=<datadog-api-key>
                    - text: ), New Relic (
                    - code [ref=e1298]: api-key=<license-key>
                    - text: ), or Grafana Cloud (
                    - code [ref=e1299]: Authorization=Basic <credentials>
                    - text: ). Only the
                    - strong [ref=e1300]: Metrics
                    - text: section accepts more than one comma-separated
                    - code [ref=e1301]: Key=Value
                    - text: pair. Refer to
                    - link "Metrics and Logs" [ref=e1302] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/metrics-and-logs/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1303]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1304]: Applied critical security fixes to improve the appliance's security posture.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e1305]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e1306] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-5"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1307]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1308]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused a blank disk hot-plugged to a running virtual machine to always use the
                    - code [ref=e1309]: ReadWriteMany
                    - text: access mode and the
                    - code [ref=e1310]: Block
                    - text: volume mode. On storage that allows only the
                    - code [ref=e1311]: Filesystem
                    - text: volume mode, the DataVolume never became ready. The
                    - strong [ref=e1312]: Create New Blank Disk
                    - text: form now has
                    - strong [ref=e1313]: Access Mode
                    - text: and
                    - strong [ref=e1314]: Volume Mode
                    - text: settings.
                    - strong [ref=e1315]: Access Mode
                    - text: defaults to
                    - code [ref=e1316]: ReadWriteMany
                    - text: ", and"
                    - strong [ref=e1317]: Volume Mode
                    - text: defaults to
                    - strong [ref=e1318]: Auto (from StorageProfile)
                    - text: ", which uses the volume mode from the StorageProfile of the StorageClass."
                - list [ref=e1319]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1320]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused a DataVolume to show the
                    - strong [ref=e1321]: Unknown
                    - text: phase, with no reason, when its PersistentVolumeClaim (PVC) could not be provisioned, such as when the storage backend was full or offline. The DataVolume now shows a warning with the reason, and its details list the DataVolume conditions and the PVC events. Uploads to a DataVolume in this state now fail with the reason instead of timing out after 2 minutes.
                - list [ref=e1322]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1323]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the confirmation dialog to grow taller than the browser window when you deleted a resource that is in use, such as a Portworx StorageClass with attached DataVolumes. This cut off the warning, the list of attached resources, and the
                    - strong [ref=e1324]: OK
                    - text: button. The dialog now fits the window, and long lists scroll.
                - list [ref=e1325]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1326]: Fixed an issue that caused long error messages to overflow their notification and hide its close button. Error notifications now also stay visible for 5 seconds.
                - heading "October 28, 2026 - Release 4.10.34Direct link to October 28, 2026 - Release 4.10.34" [level=2] [ref=e1327]:
                  - text: October 28, 2026 - Release 4.10.34
                  - link "Direct link to October 28, 2026 - Release 4.10.34" [ref=e1328] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#october-28-2026---release-41034"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1329]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1330] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-6"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1331]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1332]:
                    - text: Applied further fixes for CVE-2026-111943, the eMudhra certificate authority (CA) compromise. Provider images and Edge installer ISOs that you build with CanvOS 4.10.11 or later no longer trust the affected root certificates. Refer to
                    - link "Security Advisory 024" [ref=e1333] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /security-bulletins/security-advisories/#security-advisory-024---emudhra-certificate-authority-compromise-tls-trust-store-exposure
                    - text: for the affected deployments and recommended actions. Appliance mode Edge hosts that use images built with an earlier CanvOS version can use the workaround in
                    - link "Troubleshooting - Edge" [ref=e1334] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---appliance-mode-edge-hosts-trust-compromised-emudhra-certificate-authorities
                    - text: . The workaround applies to hosts that run Ubuntu, SUSE Linux Enterprise Micro, Red Hat Enterprise Linux, or Rocky Linux.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e1335]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e1336] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-2"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e1337]:
                  - generic [ref=e1338]:
                    - img [ref=e1340]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e1343]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e1344] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.34 Palette release is 4.10.11.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e1345]:
                  - text: Automation
                  - link "Direct link to Automation" [ref=e1346] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#automation-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e1347]:
                  - generic [ref=e1348]:
                    - img [ref=e1350]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e1353]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "Palette CLI" [ref=e1354] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /automation/palette-cli/
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.34 Palette release is 4.10.12. Refer to
                    - link "CLI Tools" [ref=e1355] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/cli-tools/
                    - text: for the download URL and checksum.
                - heading "October 24, 2026 - Release 4.10.33Direct link to October 24, 2026 - Release 4.10.33" [level=2] [ref=e1356]:
                  - text: October 24, 2026 - Release 4.10.33
                  - link "Direct link to October 24, 2026 - Release 4.10.33" [ref=e1357] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#october-24-2026---release-41033"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=3] [ref=e1358]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e1359] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-4-10-33"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1360]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1361]:
                    - text: To improve security, the
                    - code [ref=e1362]: "GET /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/assets/frpKubeconfig"
                    - text: API endpoint now requires the
                    - code [ref=e1363]: cluster.adminKubeconfigDownload
                    - text: permission, the same permission that the admin kubeconfig endpoint requires. Users who have only the
                    - code [ref=e1364]: cluster.get
                    - text: permission, such as users assigned the Cluster Viewer role, can no longer download a kubeconfig file through this endpoint. Their requests fail with the
                    - code [ref=e1365]: OperationForbidden
                    - text: error. If your automation calls this endpoint, use an API key that belongs to a user with the Cluster Admin role or a custom role that includes the
                    - code [ref=e1366]: cluster.adminKubeconfigDownload
                    - text: permission. Refer to
                    - link "Kubeconfig" [ref=e1367] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/kubeconfig/#cluster-admin
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1368]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1369] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-7"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1370]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1371]:
                    - text: Applied fixes for CVE-2026-111943, the eMudhra certificate authority (CA) compromise. Refer to
                    - link "Security Advisory 024" [ref=e1372] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /security-bulletins/security-advisories/#security-advisory-024---emudhra-certificate-authority-compromise-tls-trust-store-exposure
                    - text: for the affected deployments and recommended actions. Appliance mode Edge hosts that run Ubuntu, SUSE Linux Enterprise Micro, Red Hat Enterprise Linux, or Rocky Linux can use the workaround in
                    - link "Troubleshooting - Edge" [ref=e1373] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---appliance-mode-edge-hosts-trust-compromised-emudhra-certificate-authorities
                    - text: .
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e1374]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e1375] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-6"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1376]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1377]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where provider image downloads on Edge hosts with limited network bandwidth repeatedly failed after 45 minutes with the error
                    - code [ref=e1378]: context deadline exceeded
                    - text: . The cluster remained in the
                    - strong [ref=e1379]: Provisioning
                    - text: state, or the agent upgrade did not complete. The time limit for each download and extraction attempt is now 6 hours. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Troubleshooting - Edge" [ref=e1380] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---provider-image-download-times-out-on-slow-network-connections
                    - text: .
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e1381]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e1382] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-3"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e1383]:
                  - generic [ref=e1384]:
                    - img [ref=e1386]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e1389]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e1390] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.33 Palette release is 4.10.10.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e1391]:
                  - text: Automation

                - heading "October 23, 2026 - Component UpdatesDirect link to October 23, 2026 - Component Updates" [level=2] [ref=e1402]:
                  - text: October 23, 2026 - Component Updates
                  - link "Direct link to October 23, 2026 - Component Updates" [ref=e1403] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#component-updates-2026-43"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e1404]: The following components have been updated for Palette version 4.10.13 - 4.10.32.

                  - rowgroup [ref=e1406]:
                    - row "Component Version" [ref=e1407]:
                      - columnheader "Component" [ref=e1408]

                          - /url: /enterprise-version/install-palette/palette-management-appliance/
                      - cell "4.10.27" [ref=e1426]
                    - row "VerteX Management Appliance 4.10.27" [ref=e1427]:

                      - cell "4.10.27" [ref=e1430]
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1431]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1432] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-8"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1433]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1434]:
                    - text: Deprecated the
                    - code [ref=e1435]: override_kubeadm_configuration
                    - text: machine pool parameter for EKS, AKS, and GKE clusters in the Spectro Cloud Terraform and Crossplane providers. Palette does not support this parameter for managed Kubernetes clusters, so it has no effect. The Terraform provider now displays a warning when you set it.
                - list [ref=e1436]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1437]: Built and published Launchpad AI model recipes as standalone content archives in Artifact Studio, allowing new model support and vLLM inference engine updates to ship independently of core product release cycles.

                - heading "October 22, 2026 - Release 4.10.32Direct link to October 22, 2026 - Release 4.10.32" [level=2] [ref=e1561]:

                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1563]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1564] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-9"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1565]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1566]:
                    - text: Improved the load times of the
                    - strong [ref=e1567]: Users
                    - text: and
                    - strong [ref=e1568]: Teams
                    - text: tabs on the
                    - strong [ref=e1569]: Users & Teams
                    - text: page for tenants with a large number of
                    - link "users and teams" [ref=e1570] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /user-management/users-and-teams/
                    - text: . Palette now retrieves users and teams through the new paginated
                    - code [ref=e1571]: POST /v2/users/summary
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e1572]: POST /v2/teams/summary
                    - text: API endpoints. The
                    - code [ref=e1573]: POST /v1/users/summary
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e1574]: POST /v1/teams/summary
                    - text: endpoints continue to work as before.
