                - heading "October 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.27Direct link to October 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.27" [level=2] [ref=e1751]:
                  - text: October 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.27
                  - link "Direct link to October 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.27" [ref=e1752] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#october-15-2026---release-41027"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1753]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1754] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-11"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1755]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1756]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1757]: Strengthened the authorization checks on the Edge host APIs. Palette now verifies permissions against the specific Edge host in each request. If you call these APIs from scripts or automation, review the following changes in behavior.
                    - list [ref=e1758]:
                      - listitem [ref=e1759]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1760]:
                          - text: Registration requests to
                          - code [ref=e1761]: POST /v1/edgehosts/register
                          - text: must include a registration token in the
                          - code [ref=e1762]: hostAuthToken
                          - text: field for every Edge host type, including when you re-register an existing Edge host. Requests without a token fail with the error
                          - code [ref=e1763]: Parameter 'hostAuthToken' should not be empty
                          - text: . Include a
                          - link "registration token" [ref=e1764] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/site-installation/create-registration-token/
                          - text: associated with the Edge host's project in the request.
                      - listitem [ref=e1765]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1766]:
                          - text: System-scope roles no longer grant Edge host permissions. Use a project-scope role that includes the required
                          - code [ref=e1767]: edgeHost
                          - text: permissions instead.
                      - listitem [ref=e1768]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1769]:
                          - text: Updating the pairing key with
                          - code [ref=e1770]: "PATCH /v1/edgehosts/{uid}/hostPairingKey"
                          - text: or the checksum with
                          - code [ref=e1771]: "PATCH /v1/edgehosts/{uid}/hostCheckSum"
                          - text: requires the
                          - code [ref=e1772]: edgeHost.update
                          - text: permission on the Edge host in its project. The Tenant Admin role alone no longer grants this access outside the Edge host's project context.
                      - listitem [ref=e1773]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1774]:
                          - text: Resetting an Edge host with
                          - code [ref=e1775]: "PUT /v1/edgehosts/{uid}/reset"
                          - text: requires the
                          - code [ref=e1776]: edgeHost.get
                          - text: permission on the Edge host, as well as the existing cluster permission.
                      - listitem [ref=e1777]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1778]:
                          - code [ref=e1779]: "GET /v1/edgehosts/{uid}"
                          - text: now returns a masked value for the temporary SSH user's key in the
                          - code [ref=e1780]: sshKey
                          - text: field. Remote Shell sessions that you open in Palette are not affected.
                    - paragraph [ref=e1781]:
                      - text: Edge hosts that register with a valid registration token are not affected. Apart from the masked
                      - code [ref=e1782]: sshKey
                      - text: value, users with the Project Admin, Project Editor, or Tenant Admin role who work in the Edge host's project are also not affected. For more information, refer to
                      - link "Edge Host Registration" [ref=e1783] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/site-installation/edge-host-registration/
                      - text: .
                - list [ref=e1784]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1785]: Applied security fixes to improve the platform's security posture.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e1786]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e1787] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-7"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1788]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1789]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where changing the number of nodes in a node pool from the cluster's
                    - strong [ref=e1790]: Nodes
                    - text: tab turned off the
                    - link "Skip worker node update (Optional)" [ref=e1791] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#skip-worker-node-update
                      - strong [ref=e1792]: Skip worker node update (Optional)
                    - text: option for that node pool. As a result, the next Kubernetes upgrade also upgraded the node pool's worker nodes. Changing the node count by editing the node pool configuration did not turn off the option.
                - list [ref=e1793]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1794]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where the
                    - strong [ref=e1795]: Teams
                    - text: tab on the
                    - strong [ref=e1796]: Users & Teams
                    - text: page did not load for tenants with 100 or more
                    - link "teams" [ref=e1797] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /user-management/users-and-teams/create-a-team/
                    - text: . The tab stayed on a loading indicator, and the user service logs showed the error
                    - code [ref=e1798]: BSONObj exceeds maximum nested object depth
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1799]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1800]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where control plane updates failed on clusters whose Kubernetes pack
                    - code [ref=e1801]: extraArgs
                    - text: include an unquoted integer of 1,000,000 or greater, such as
                    - code [ref=e1802]: "quota-backend-bytes: 4294967296"
                    - text: . After an upgrade to Palette agent version 4.10, the next cluster profile or pack change converted the value to scientific notation, such as
                    - code [ref=e1803]: "4.294967296e+09"
                    - text: . The new control plane nodes then failed to join the cluster with the error
                    - code [ref=e1804]: "etcd-join: the etcd member is not started"
                    - text: . Palette now preserves integer values. Affected clusters
                    - link "repave" [ref=e1805] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#repave-behavior-and-configuration
                    - text: their control plane nodes once after the upgrade to apply the corrected values.
                - list [ref=e1806]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1807]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where the Palette Management Appliance and VerteX Management Appliance could not deploy new clusters when an administrator turned on
                    - strong [ref=e1808]: Enable Certificate Rotation
                    - text: for the internal Zot registry after upgrading from version 4.9. Cluster deployments failed with the error
                    - code [ref=e1809]: "failed to get in-cluster registry: in-cluster registry credential not found"
                    - text: . Palette and Palette VerteX now update the registry certificate authority (CA) automatically, so the registry CA update script is required only on version 4.10.25. Refer to the Upgrade Notes for
                    - link "Palette" [ref=e1810] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/upgrade/palette-management-appliance/#upgrade-notes
                    - text: and
                    - link "Palette VerteX" [ref=e1811] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vertex/upgrade/vertex-management-appliance/#upgrade-notes
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e1812]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1813]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where Palette kept duplicate machine records for an Edge host after its node re-registered with a new Kubernetes node UID, for example, after running
                    - code [ref=e1814]: kubectl delete node
                    - text: and restarting the Kubelet. The duplicate records appeared as extra nodes in Palette, and removing an outdated record could release the Edge host from its cluster and reset it. Palette now rejects a second machine record for an Edge host that already has a running machine in the same node pool, and the Palette agent no longer deletes the machine record of a running node. Palette also removes existing duplicate machine records automatically after the upgrade.
                - list [ref=e1815]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1816]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue on clusters that use a proxy server, including Edge clusters, where Palette task pods remained in the
                    - code [ref=e1817]: Terminating
                    - text: state and their tasks did not complete. Affected tasks include collecting node logs, applying OS patches, and updating NTP and SSH key settings, and the task jobs reported the error
                    - code [ref=e1818]: pod updates may not change fields other than
                    - text: . Palette now injects proxy settings, such as environment variables and CA certificates, only when a pod is created. Palette also keeps a pod's existing pod affinity and pod anti-affinity rules when it adds node affinity rules.
                - list [ref=e1819]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1820]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue on
                    - link "two-node" [ref=e1821] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/architecture/two-node/
                    - text: Edge clusters where, after a failover, a demoted node could stop replicating data from the leader while the cluster continued to report a healthy status. A later failover could then promote the node with out-of-date data. Palette now stops the node's local Kubernetes and datastore processes before demoting it. Palette also generates an Error audit event when replication stalls and an Info audit event when replication recovers.
                - list [ref=e1822]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1823]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue on locally managed Edge clusters and Edge clusters that use Canonical Kubernetes where the node's
                    - code [ref=e1824]: <node>-public-key
                    - text: secret in the
                    - code [ref=e1825]: spectro-system
                    - text: namespace was not created if bootstrap hit a temporary failure, such as DNS or the Kubernetes API server not being ready. The task still reported success. Services that validate
                    - link "Local UI" [ref=e1826] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/
                    - text: tokens then rejected users on that node, and
                    - code [ref=e1827]: /var/log/public-key-manager.log
                    - text: showed the error
                    - code [ref=e1828]: Failed to create secret <node>-public-key
                    - text: . The task now retries until the secret exists.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e1829]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e1830] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-4"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e1831]:
                  - generic [ref=e1832]:
                    - img [ref=e1834]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e1837]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e1838] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.27 Palette release is 4.10.9.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e1839]:
                  - text: Automation
                  - link "Direct link to Automation" [ref=e1840] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#automation-3"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e1841]:
                  - generic [ref=e1842]:
                    - img [ref=e1844]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e1847]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "Palette CLI" [ref=e1848] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /automation/palette-cli/
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.27 Palette release is 4.10.10. Refer to
                    - link "CLI Tools" [ref=e1849] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/cli-tools/
                    - text: for the download URL and checksum.
                - heading "October 9, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.20Direct link to October 9, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.20" [level=2] [ref=e1850]:
                  - text: October 9, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.20
                  - link "Direct link to October 9, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.20" [ref=e1851] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vm-launchpad-4.10.20"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e1852]: This release delivers version 4.10.20 of the PaletteAI VM Launchpad appliance, which runs on Palette 4.10.25.
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=3] [ref=e1853]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e1854] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-vm-launchpad-4.10.20"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1855]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1856]:
                    - text: Only the Platform Admin VMO role can now create, edit, and delete cluster-scoped instance types and preferences. Users with the Editor role can still view and select them, but can no longer change them. Assign the Platform Admin role to users who manage cluster-scoped instance types and preferences. Refer to
                    - link "VMO Roles" [ref=e1857] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/access-management/vmo-roles/
                    - text: .
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=3] [ref=e1858]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e1859] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-5"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1860]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1861]:
                    - text: PaletteAI VM Launchpad can now forward appliance metrics and logs to backends that accept the OpenTelemetry Protocol (OTLP) over HTTP, as well as to Splunk HTTP Event Collector (HEC). The
                    - strong [ref=e1862]: Forwarding URL
                    - text: determines the protocol. The
                    - strong [ref=e1863]: Logs
                    - text: section of the
                    - strong [ref=e1864]: Metrics and Logs
                    - text: page now controls log delivery and has its own
                    - strong [ref=e1865]: Forwarding Token
                    - text: and
                    - strong [ref=e1866]: CA Certificate
                    - text: settings. Both sections have a new
                    - strong [ref=e1867]: Skip TLS Verification
                    - text: setting, which is off by default. When
                    - strong [ref=e1868]: Metrics Forwarding
                    - text: is enabled, the appliance also forwards its logs to the metrics receiver unless you configure the
                    - strong [ref=e1869]: Logs
                    - text: section. Refer to
                    - link "Metrics and Logs" [ref=e1870] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/metrics-and-logs/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1871]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1872]:
                    - text: Portworx appliances now support Everpure FlashArray as a storage backend for the Portworx storage cluster. Refer to
                    - link "Storage" [ref=e1873] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/infrastructure/storage/#storage-clusters
                    - text: .
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e1874]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e1875] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-12"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1876]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1877]: Non-FIPS appliances now use hardened images for the MetalLB, Local Path Provisioner, Traefik, Piraeus, and Registry Connect packs, and for the VMO pack components where a hardened image is available.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e1878]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e1879] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-8"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1880]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1881]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused namespaces enabled for virtual machines to disappear from the VMO UI after an update to the VMO pack, leaving only the
                    - code [ref=e1882]: default
                    - text: namespace.
                - list [ref=e1883]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1884]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the namespace list in the
                    - strong [ref=e1885]: Upload ISO
                    - text: form to include namespaces that the user could not access, so the upload failed with a permission error after it started. The namespace lists in the
                    - strong [ref=e1886]: Upload ISO
                    - text: and
                    - strong [ref=e1887]: Build Golden Image
                    - text: forms now show only namespaces that the user can access.
                - list [ref=e1888]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1889]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented single-node non-FIPS Piraeus appliances from storing images in the appliance registry, so cluster bootstrap failed and pods remained in
                    - code [ref=e1890]: ImagePullBackOff
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1891]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1892]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that hid the sub-steps and tips of a
                    - strong [ref=e1893]: Getting Started
                    - text: wizard step after you completed the step. You can now reopen them.
                - list [ref=e1894]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1895]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused VMO to delete uploaded images about 30 minutes after the upload started. VMO no longer deletes uploads that remain in the
                    - code [ref=e1896]: UploadReady
                    - text: phase, so remove abandoned uploads manually.
                - list [ref=e1897]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1898]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - strong [ref=e1899]: Create VM
                    - text: wizard to create a
                    - code [ref=e1900]: Filesystem
                    - text: PersistentVolumeClaim (PVC) instead of a
                    - code [ref=e1901]: Block
                    - text: PVC when the root disk of a virtual machine template imports from a container registry. The disk import failed with
                    - code [ref=e1902]: "qemu-img: Could not create '/data/disk.img': Permission denied"
                    - text: ", or the PVC remained"
                    - code [ref=e1903]: Pending
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1904]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1905]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused virtual machines created from an edited template to fail with
                    - code [ref=e1906]: disks[0].Name 'datavolume-os' not found
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e1907]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1908]: Fixed an issue that prevented virtual machines on non-FIPS Portworx appliances from attaching volumes from a ReadWriteMany (RWX) storage class, which also caused live migration to fail. The appliance image now includes the NFS packages that Portworx requires, for both new installations and upgraded appliances.
                - list [ref=e1909]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1910]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that marked the
                    - strong [ref=e1911]: Set Up a Storage Class
                    - text: step of the
                    - strong [ref=e1912]: Getting Started
                    - text: wizard as complete when you opened the
                    - strong [ref=e1913]: Create Storage Class
                    - text: wizard and canceled it without creating a StorageClass.
                - list [ref=e1914]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1915]: Fixed an issue that caused VMO Manager to run out of memory and restart when multiple users logged in at the same time. Each restart logged out every user.
                - list [ref=e1916]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1917]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused a stopped virtual machine to show
                    - strong [ref=e1918]: Stopping
                    - text: indefinitely, with no error, when its disk could not be provisioned. The virtual machine now shows
                    - strong [ref=e1919]: Stopped
                    - text: ", and a banner explains why the disk cannot be provisioned."
                - heading "October 9, 2026 - Release 4.10.25Direct link to October 9, 2026 - Release 4.10.25" [level=2] [ref=e1920]:
                  - text: October 9, 2026 - Release 4.10.25
                  - link "Direct link to October 9, 2026 - Release 4.10.25" [ref=e1921] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#release-notes-4.10.a"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Security NoticesDirect link to Security Notices" [level=3] [ref=e1922]:
                  - text: Security Notices
                  - link "Direct link to Security Notices" [ref=e1923] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#security-notices-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1924]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1925]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1926]:
                      - text: To improve the security posture of using Palette as an identity provider, Palette now validates the callback URL on every authentication request. Refer to
                      - link "Register OIDC Callback URLs" [ref=e1927] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /user-management/saml-sso/register-oidc-callback-urls/
                      - text: for more information.
                  - listitem [ref=e1928]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1929]:
                      - text: Review the
                      - link "Security Bulletins" [ref=e1930] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /security-bulletins/reports/
                      - text: page for the latest security advisories.
                - heading "Palette EnterpriseDirect link to Palette Enterprise" [level=3] [ref=e1931]:
                  - text: Palette Enterprise
                  - link "Direct link to Palette Enterprise" [ref=e1932] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#palette-enterprise-4.10.a"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=4] [ref=e1933]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e1934] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-4.10.a"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e1935]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1936]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1937]: Palette now enforces authorization checks on nine APIs that previously required no permission. Automation that calls these endpoints must use a principal that holds the required permission. Otherwise, the request fails with an authorization error.
                    - table [ref=e1938]:
                      - rowgroup [ref=e1939]:
                        - row "API Required permission" [ref=e1940]:
                          - columnheader "API" [ref=e1941]
                          - columnheader "Required permission" [ref=e1942]
                      - rowgroup [ref=e1943]:
                        - 'row "PATCH /v1/cloudaccounts/{uid}/geoLocation Update permission on the cloud account" [ref=e1944]':
                          - 'cell "PATCH /v1/cloudaccounts/{uid}/geoLocation" [ref=e1945]':
                            - code [ref=e1946]: "PATCH /v1/cloudaccounts/{uid}/geoLocation"
                          - cell "Update permission on the cloud account" [ref=e1947]
                        - 'row "POST /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/workloads/sync Update permission on the cluster" [ref=e1948]':
                          - 'cell "POST /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/workloads/sync" [ref=e1949]':
                            - code [ref=e1950]: "POST /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/workloads/sync"
                          - cell "Update permission on the cluster" [ref=e1951]
                        - 'row "GET /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/edge-native/edgeHosts Get permission on the cluster" [ref=e1952]':
                          - 'cell "GET /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/edge-native/edgeHosts" [ref=e1953]':
                            - code [ref=e1954]: "GET /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/edge-native/edgeHosts"
                          - cell "Get permission on the cluster" [ref=e1955]
                        - row "GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMapping Get permission on the DNS mapping object" [ref=e1956]:
                          - cell "GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMapping" [ref=e1957]:
                            - code [ref=e1958]: GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMapping
                          - cell "Get permission on the DNS mapping object" [ref=e1959]
                        - row "GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMappings List permission on DNS mapping objects" [ref=e1960]:
                          - cell "GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMappings" [ref=e1961]:
                            - code [ref=e1962]: GET /v1/users/assets/vsphere/dnsMappings
                          - cell "List permission on DNS mapping objects" [ref=e1963]
                        - row "GET /v1/registries/oci/summary List permission on registries" [ref=e1964]:
                          - cell "GET /v1/registries/oci/summary" [ref=e1965]:
                            - code [ref=e1966]: GET /v1/registries/oci/summary
                          - cell "List permission on registries" [ref=e1967]
                        - 'row "GET /v1/packs/{packName}/registries/{registryUid} Get permission on the registry" [ref=e1968]':
                          - 'cell "GET /v1/packs/{packName}/registries/{registryUid}" [ref=e1969]':
                            - code [ref=e1970]: "GET /v1/packs/{packName}/registries/{registryUid}"
                          - cell "Get permission on the registry" [ref=e1971]
                        - 'row "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/metadata Tenant Admin role on the tenant" [ref=e1972]':
                          - 'cell "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/metadata" [ref=e1973]':
                            - code [ref=e1974]: "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/metadata"
                          - cell "Tenant Admin role on the tenant" [ref=e1975]
                        - 'row "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/aws/{uid} Tenant Admin role on the tenant" [ref=e1976]':
                          - 'cell "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/aws/{uid}" [ref=e1977]':
                            - code [ref=e1978]: "GET /v1/tenants/{tenantUid}/subscriptions/aws/{uid}"
                          - cell "Tenant Admin role on the tenant" [ref=e1979]
                    - paragraph [ref=e1980]:
                      - text: The related endpoint
                      - code [ref=e1981]: "POST /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/workloads/{kind}/sync"
                      - text: ", which syncs a single workload kind, requires the same Update permission on the cluster as"
                      - code [ref=e1982]: "POST /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/workloads/sync"
                      - text: in the table above.
                - list [ref=e1983]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1984]:
                    - text: To improve security, virtual machine operations now also require the
                    - code [ref=e1985]: cluster.get
                    - text: permission on the cluster that hosts the virtual machine (
                    - code [ref=e1986]: virtualCluster.get
                    - text: for virtual clusters). Users who have a virtual machine role, such as Virtual Machine Power User or Virtual Machine User, but no role that includes
                    - code [ref=e1987]: cluster.get
                    - text: in the cluster's project can no longer view or manage the virtual machines on that cluster. Assign these users the Cluster Viewer role, or a custom role that includes
                    - code [ref=e1988]: cluster.get
                    - text: ", in the cluster's project. If you upgrade from"
                    - link "Palette 4.10.17-patch.5" [ref=e1989] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: "#breaking-changes-4-10-17-patch-5"
                    - text: ", operations other than viewing no longer require the"
                    - code [ref=e1990]: cluster.update
                    - text: permission. Users who have the Cluster Editor role only for virtual machine operations now need only the Cluster Viewer role. Refer to
                    - link "VM User Roles and Permissions" [ref=e1991] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/rbac/vm-roles-permissions/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e1992]:
                  - listitem [ref=e1993]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e1994]: Palette now validates the callback URL on every authentication request when it acts as an identity provider (IdP). Earlier releases accepted any callback URL. A tenant admin must register callback URLs that Palette does not allow by default. Authentication that succeeded before the upgrade fails until a tenant admin registers the callback URL, with some exceptions.
                    - paragraph [ref=e1995]: You do not need to register the callback URLs after upgrade in the following situations.
                    - list [ref=e1996]:
                      - listitem [ref=e1997]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e1998]: The callback URL is on your Palette domain.
                      - listitem [ref=e1999]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e2000]:
                          - text: The callback URL is on the local machine, such as
                          - code [ref=e2001]: localhost
                          - text: or
                          - code [ref=e2002]: 127.0.0.1
                          - text: .
                      - listitem [ref=e2003]:
                        - paragraph [ref=e2004]: The callback URL belongs to a Virtual Machine Orchestrator or VM Migration Assistant cluster that exists at the time of the upgrade with its address set to a literal value or a variable.
                        - paragraph [ref=e2005]:
                          - text: Palette registers the Virtual Machine Orchestrator and VM Migration Assistant callback URLs automatically at upgrade, including an address set with a variable. However, Palette cannot resolve an address set with a
                          - link "macro" [ref=e2006] [cursor=pointer]:
                            - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/macros/
                          - text: ", so sign-in to those clusters fails until a tenant admin registers the address. If you change a cluster's callback URL after the upgrade, you must register the new value."
                    - paragraph [ref=e2007]:
                      - text: For more information, refer to
                      - link "Register OIDC Callback URLs" [ref=e2008] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /user-management/saml-sso/register-oidc-callback-urls/
                      - text: .
                - list [ref=e2009]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2010]:
                    - text: To improve security, Palette SaaS now rejects Splunk audit trail endpoints that point at private or reserved IP addresses. Palette checks the HTTP Event Collector (HEC) URL when you create, update, or validate a Splunk audit trail, and each time it sends audit logs to Splunk. Palette rejects a URL that is, or resolves to, a private (RFC 1918), localhost, link-local, multicast, carrier-grade NAT (
                    - code [ref=e2011]: 100.64.0.0/10
                    - text: ), or unspecified address, including the cloud instance metadata address
                    - code [ref=e2012]: 169.254.169.254
                    - text: ", as well as a hostname that it cannot resolve. A rejected request returns the"
                    - code [ref=e2013]: InvalidParam
                    - text: error code, and an existing audit trail that points at a blocked address stops delivering logs after the upgrade. Self-hosted Palette and Palette VerteX are not affected. If you use Palette SaaS with a Splunk instance on an internal IP address, expose a publicly routable HEC endpoint and update the audit trail to use it. Refer to
                    - link "Push Audit Trails to Amazon CloudWatch or Splunk" [ref=e2014] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /audit-logs/#push-audit-trails-to-amazon-cloudwatch-or-splunk
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2015]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2016]:
                    - text: Palette now requires a role name when you create, update, or clone a role. A request with an empty or whitespace-only
                    - code [ref=e2017]: metadata.name
                    - text: fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2018]: InvalidName
                    - text: error code. Palette does not modify existing roles, but you must add a name to any unnamed role the next time you update it. Update any automation that creates or modifies roles through the API and might pass an unset name. The Spectro Cloud Terraform provider already requires a role name, so this change does not affect Terraform configurations.
                - list [ref=e2019]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2020]:
                    - text: Registry names must now be unique when you rename a pack or Helm registry, consistent with the existing check when you create a registry. Renaming a registry to a name that another registry in the same tenant already uses through
                    - code [ref=e2021]: "PUT /v1/registries/pack/{uid}"
                    - text: or
                    - code [ref=e2022]: "PUT /v1/registries/helm/{uid}"
                    - text: fails with an HTTP 409 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2023]: ResourceNameAlreadyExists
                    - text: error code. Name matching is case-insensitive and applies across all registry types, so a pack registry cannot take the name of a Helm or OCI registry. If two registries already share a name, rename one of them to a unique name. Update any automation or Terraform configuration that renames registries so that it uses unique names.
                - list [ref=e2024]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2025]:
                    - text: The private and synchronization settings of a Helm registry are now fixed when you create the registry, because Palette determines synchronization support from the private setting at creation. The Palette UI no longer offers the
                    - strong [ref=e2026]: Synchronization
                    - text: toggle when you edit an existing Helm registry. A
                    - code [ref=e2027]: "PUT /v1/registries/helm/{uid}"
                    - text: request that changes
                    - code [ref=e2028]: spec.isPrivate
                    - text: fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2029]: HelmRegistryImmutableField
                    - text: error code. A request that omits
                    - code [ref=e2030]: isPrivate
                    - text: is treated as public, so an update to a private registry must include
                    - code [ref=e2031]: "\"isPrivate\": true"
                    - text: . To change either setting, delete the registry, add it again with the setting you need, and update any cluster profiles that reference it. Refer to
                    - link "Synchronization Behavior" [ref=e2032] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/helm-charts/#synchronization-behavior
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2033]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2034]:
                    - text: K3s is no longer a supported distribution for virtual clusters and cluster groups. You cannot create new K3s cluster groups, and you cannot upgrade existing K3s cluster groups to vCluster 0.36.x. Existing K3s virtual clusters continue to run on their current vCluster version. To move to vCluster 0.36.x, create a new Kubernetes-based cluster group and migrate your workloads. Refer to
                    - link "Upgrade Cluster Groups" [ref=e2035] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-groups/vcluster-upgrades/#upgrade-cluster-group
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "Upgrade NotesDirect link to Upgrade Notes" [level=4] [ref=e2036]:
                  - text: Upgrade Notes
                  - link "Direct link to Upgrade Notes" [ref=e2037] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#upgrade-notes-4.10.a"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2038]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2039]:
                    - strong [ref=e2040]: "User action required:"
                    - text: The Palette Management Appliance and VerteX Management Appliance can now rotate the TLS certificate of their internal Zot registry automatically. Rotation is turned off by default when you upgrade. After you upgrade, resume any paused agent upgrades, wait for the Palette agent upgrade to complete, turn on
                    - strong [ref=e2041]: Enable Certificate Rotation
                    - text: in the Local UI, and then run the registry certificate authority (CA) update script. If you leave rotation turned off, the registry certificate eventually expires, unless you rotate it manually. Refer to the Upgrade Notes for
                    - link "Palette" [ref=e2042] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/upgrade/palette-management-appliance/#upgrade-notes
                    - text: and
                    - link "Palette VerteX" [ref=e2043] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vertex/upgrade/vertex-management-appliance/#upgrade-notes
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2044]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2045]:
                    - text: "Self-hosted Palette and VerteX now warn you to configure the image pull secret before you upgrade or install. If your installation pulls images from the Spectro Cloud registry instead of a local registry and the secret is not configured, Local UI (for appliances) and the system console (for Enterprise Clusters) display a warning. The warning is not enforced and does not block the upgrade or installation: the installation stays available and existing workload clusters are unaffected. However, new cluster deployments and day-2 operations that pull Spectro Cloud images fail until you configure the pull secret. Airgapped installations and installations that use a mirrored registry are not affected. For more information, refer to"
                    - link "Configure Image Pull Secret" [ref=e2046] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/system-management/configure-image-pull-secret/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2047]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2048]:
                    - text: If you have cluster groups on the K3s distribution, you cannot upgrade them to vCluster 0.36.x or later, because K3s is no longer a supported virtual cluster distribution. Existing K3s virtual clusters continue to run on their current vCluster version. Refer to
                    - link "Upgrade Cluster Groups" [ref=e2049] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-groups/vcluster-upgrades/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2050]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2051]:
                    - text: If your EKS clusters use the
                    - code [ref=e2052]: irsaRoles
                    - text: field of the EKS pack, add the
                    - code [ref=e2053]: iam:ListRoles
                    - text: action to the
                    - strong [ref=e2054]: PaletteControllersEKSPolicy
                    - text: AWS IAM policy. Palette requires this action to delete the IAM Roles for Service Accounts (IRSA) roles it manages when you delete an EKS cluster. IRSA roles of clusters deleted without this permission remain in your AWS account. You can identify them by the
                    - code [ref=e2055]: palette.spectrocloud.com/cluster-uid
                    - text: tag and delete them manually. Refer to
                    - link "Controllers EKS Policy" [ref=e2056] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/required-iam-policies/additional-iam-policies-specific-use-cases/#controllers-eks-policy
                    - text: for the updated policy.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e2057]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e2058] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-6"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2059]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2060]:
                    - text: EKS and GKE clusters now support dedicating a worker node pool for system pods, both Palette and non-Palette. Select
                    - strong [ref=e2061]: Dedicate node pool for system pods
                    - text: in the pool configuration to keep non-system workloads off the pool, reserving the other node pools in the cluster for your own workloads. Refer to
                    - link "Dedicated node pool for system pods" [ref=e2062] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#dedicated-system-pod-pool
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2063]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2064]:
                    - text: You can now attach a running, Palette-provisioned cluster to an existing cluster template through the Palette UI or the Spectro Cloud Terraform provider, bringing the cluster under template governance for future upgrades and policy enforcement. Refer to
                    - link "Attach an Existing Cluster to a Cluster Template" [ref=e2065] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /cluster-templates/attach-cluster-to-template/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2066]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2067]:
                    - text: Self-hosted Palette and VerteX can now export platform metrics to an external observability stack, such as Splunk, using an OpenTelemetry collector. You configure and manage the export from the
                    - strong [ref=e2068]: Metrics
                    - text: tab in the system console. For more information, refer to
                    - link "Export Platform Metrics" [ref=e2069] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/system-management/export-metrics/
                    - text: .
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e2070]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2071] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-13"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2072]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2073]: Beneath the pull secret configuration field, the system console now shows who configured the image pull secret and when.
                - list [ref=e2074]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2075]:
                    - text: When adding a worker node pool to a running cluster, you can now click
                    - strong [ref=e2076]: Copy from Control Plane Pool
                    - text: to reuse the control plane pool's configuration in the new pool. The copied fields remain editable.
                - list [ref=e2077]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2078]:
                    - text: Palette upgraded the Cluster API providers that it uses internally, including the AWS, vSphere, Azure, and GCP providers. For the current provider versions, refer to
                    - link "Cluster API Versions" [ref=e2079] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /architecture/orchestration-spectrocloud/#cluster-api-versions
                    - text: .
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e2080]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2081] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-9"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2082]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2083]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused
                    - link "cluster backups" [ref=e2084] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/backup-restore/create-cluster-backup/
                    - text: that Velero reported as
                    - code [ref=e2085]: PartiallyFailed
                    - text: to display as
                    - strong [ref=e2086]: Completed
                    - text: in Palette. These backups now display as
                    - strong [ref=e2087]: Partially Failed
                    - text: in the
                    - strong [ref=e2088]: Backups
                    - text: tab, where you can still delete or restore them.
                - list [ref=e2089]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2090]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused
                    - strong [ref=e2091]: Additional Labels
                    - text: configured on
                    - link "EKS" [ref=e2092] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: worker node pools to reach new nodes only after the nodes joined the cluster. Workloads that selected nodes by these labels could fail to schedule, and node pool updates could time out while draining nodes. Palette now applies the labels when it creates the nodes.
                - list [ref=e2093]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2094]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - code [ref=e2095]: AWSManagedMachinePool
                    - text: resource of
                    - link "EKS" [ref=e2096] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: worker node pools to list each Availability Zone twice when the node pool specified both Availability Zones and subnets.
                - list [ref=e2097]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2098]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that could prevent Palette from applying
                    - link "add-on pack changes" [ref=e2099] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cluster-updates/
                    - text: to EKS clusters and other clusters with managed worker node pools after a worker node pool recovered from a cloud provider failure, such as a deleted EC2 launch template version.
                - list [ref=e2100]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2101]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Palette to retry a
                    - link "pack" [ref=e2102] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cluster-updates/
                    - text: uninstall indefinitely when the uninstall could not complete, for example, when removing the VMO pack from a cluster that still had virtual machines. Palette now stops after 12 failed attempts and reports a pack error with the reason
                    - code [ref=e2103]: PackUninstallStalled
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2104]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2105]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Palette to unexpectedly
                    - link "repave" [ref=e2106] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#repave-behavior-and-configuration
                    - text: all control plane nodes after a cluster change that should not trigger a repave. This affected clusters first deployed with Palette 4.7 or earlier whose Palette agent was upgraded to version 4.8 or later. The agent upgrade itself did not trigger the repave. Instead, the repave started with the next cluster change, such as updating
                    - code [ref=e2107]: clientConfig.oidc-client-secret
                    - text: in the Kubernetes pack values. With this fix, these changes no longer repave control plane nodes.
                - list [ref=e2108]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2109]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused sustained high CPU usage by the
                    - code [ref=e2110]: palette-controller-manager
                    - text: pod in clusters with add-on packs from the legacy
                    - strong [ref=e2111]: Public Repo
                    - link "registry" [ref=e2112] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e2113]: registry.spectrocloud.com
                    - text: . Palette reconciled these packs every few seconds even when their configuration had not changed. Add-on packs from OCI registries were not affected.
                - list [ref=e2114]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2115]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused
                    - link "audit trail" [ref=e2116] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /audit-logs/#push-audit-trails-to-amazon-cloudwatch-or-splunk
                    - text: events pushed to Splunk to omit the event time from the event body, so records exported or forwarded from Splunk did not show when the action occurred. The event body now includes a
                    - code [ref=e2117]: timestamp
                    - text: field in UTC.
                - heading "Deprecations and RemovalsDirect link to Deprecations and Removals" [level=4] [ref=e2118]:
                  - text: Deprecations and Removals
                  - link "Direct link to Deprecations and Removals" [ref=e2119] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#deprecations-and-removals-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2120]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2121]:
                    - text: EKS Hybrid Nodes, deprecated in May 2026, are now disabled. Hybrid node configuration controls are greyed out in the Palette UI on imported EKS clusters, and API requests that enable or configure hybrid nodes are rejected. Existing clusters with hybrid nodes continue to operate, but you cannot add or modify hybrid configuration. Deploy your workloads to
                    - link "EKS clusters" [ref=e2122] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: instead.
                - list [ref=e2123]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2124]:
                    - text: The MicroK8s pack is no longer available in Palette and Palette VerteX. Use
                    - link "Canonical Kubernetes (CK8s)" [ref=e2125] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /integrations/packs/?pack=kubernetes-ck8s
                    - text: instead.
                - list [ref=e2126]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2127]: K3s is no longer a supported distribution for Palette and Palette VerteX virtual clusters or cluster groups. Palette and Palette VerteX virtual clusters now run on vCluster 0.36.x, and upstream vCluster removed K3s in 0.33. Existing K3s virtual clusters continue to run on their current vCluster version, but their cluster groups cannot be upgraded to newer vCluster versions. To move to a newer vCluster version, create a new Kubernetes-based cluster group and migrate your workloads.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e2128]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e2129] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-5"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e2130]:
                  - generic [ref=e2131]:
                    - img [ref=e2133]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e2136]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e2137] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.25 Palette release is 4.10.8.
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=4] [ref=e2138]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e2139] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-edge-4.10.a"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2140]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2141]:
                    - text: Palette now validates the control plane virtual IP (VIP) address of Edge Native clusters on cluster creation and day-2 updates. When the control plane endpoint type is VIP, the host must be a valid IPv4 address or a Fully Qualified Domain Name (FQDN). Otherwise, the request fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2142]: InvalidAddress
                    - text: error code. After a cluster is provisioned, you cannot change its control plane endpoint type or host. A day-2 update through
                    - code [ref=e2143]: "PUT /v1/cloudconfigs/edge-native/{configUid}/clusterConfig"
                    - text: that changes either value fails with an HTTP 403 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2144]: ControlPlaneEndpointUpdateForbidden
                    - text: error code. The Palette UI already enforces these rules. Update any Terraform configuration or automation that passes an invalid VIP or changes the VIP of a provisioned Edge Native cluster.
                - list [ref=e2145]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2146]:
                    - text: When you update an Edge Native node pool through the
                    - code [ref=e2147]: "PUT /v1/cloudconfigs/edge-native/{configUid}/machinePools/{machinePoolName}"
                    - text: endpoint, the number of entries in
                    - code [ref=e2148]: cloudConfig.edgeHosts
                    - text: must now match
                    - code [ref=e2149]: poolConfig.size
                    - text: . This safeguard prevents a partial host list from removing Edge hosts from the pool unintentionally, and applies to control plane and worker node pools. A request whose host count does not match the pool size fails with an HTTP 400 response and the
                    - code [ref=e2150]: ShouldBeEqualToValue
                    - text: error code. If your automation updates node-level taints or labels on some of the hosts in a pool, include every host in the pool in the request. To remove a host, reduce
                    - code [ref=e2151]: poolConfig.size
                    - text: and omit the host from
                    - code [ref=e2152]: cloudConfig.edgeHosts
                    - text: . Refer to
                    - link "Node-level Taints and Labels for Edge Native" [ref=e2153] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#node-level-taints-and-labels-for-edge-native
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e2154]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e2155] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-7"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2156]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2157]:
                    - text: On connected Edge Native clusters, you can now assign taints and labels to individual Edge hosts, so hosts in the same node pool can play different roles without needing separate pools. Set the values on each host row inside a pool's
                    - strong [ref=e2158]: Edge Hosts
                    - text: section, either during cluster creation or when you edit a pool on an existing cluster. This supports patterns such as a three-node control plane where two hosts run application workloads and a third, lightweight host participates only in etcd quorum. Refer to
                    - link "Node-level Taints and Labels for Edge Native" [ref=e2159] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/node-pool/#node-level-taints-and-labels-for-edge-native
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e2160]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2161]:
                    - text: The Palette UI now supports adding a temporary fourth control plane host to an Edge cluster node pool, so you can replace a control plane Edge host by adding the replacement before you remove the original and maintain etcd quorum throughout the operation. While the node pool contains four control plane hosts, Palette displays a warning that the cluster is in a temporary intermediate state. Refer to
                    - link "Replace a Control Plane Edge Host" [ref=e2162] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/control-plane-host-replacement/
                    - text: for the procedure, including how to verify etcd membership health before you remove the original host.
                - list [ref=e2163]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2164]:
                    - img "Technical preview feature badge" [ref=e2165]
                    - text: Connected Edge clusters now support Hadron, Spectro Cloud's minimal, immutable Edge operating system built on the CNCF Kairos project. Use the EdgeForge workflow to build Hadron-based installer ISOs and provider images. Refer to
                    - link "Build Hadron Images" [ref=e2166] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edgeforge-workflow/palette-canvos/build-provider-images/build-hadron-image/
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e2167]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2168] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-14"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2169]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2170]:
                    - text: The HTTPS certificate that Edge hosts generate for
                    - link "Local UI" [ref=e2171] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/
                    - text: is now valid for 10 years instead of 5 years. Edge hosts that already have a Local UI certificate keep their existing certificate.
                - list [ref=e2172]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2173]:
                    - text: The
                    - strong [ref=e2174]: Edge Hosts
                    - text: page now shows the Palette agent version installed on each Edge host and indicates when a newer version is available, so you can determine which hosts are behind and the version they would upgrade to. Refer to
                    - link "Identify the Latest Palette Agent Version" [ref=e2175] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/agent-upgrade-airgap/#identify-the-latest-palette-agent-version
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e2176]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2177] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-10"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2178]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2179]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where the Edge
                    - link "Local UI" [ref=e2180] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/host-management/access-console/
                    - text: reported an expired OS password as incorrect credentials at login. The login page now detects an expired password, whether an administrator or a PAM policy expired it, and lets you change the password there instead of requiring SSH or Palette TUI console access.
                - list [ref=e2181]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2182]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented add-on packs whose name contains a forward slash, such as packs sourced from a namespace in an
                    - link "OCI registry" [ref=e2183] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/oci-registry/
                    - text: ", from deploying to Edge clusters. The cluster reported"
                    - code [ref=e2184]: FailedToDownloadPack
                    - text: events with
                    - code [ref=e2185]: failed to locate pack
                    - text: errors.
                - list [ref=e2186]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2187]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Kubernetes to fail to start, with errors such as
                    - code [ref=e2188]: "bind: cannot assign requested address"
                    - text: ", after a single-node Edge cluster with an"
                    - link "overlay network" [ref=e2189] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/networking/vxlan-overlay/
                    - text: rebooted while the Edge host could not connect to Palette.
                - list [ref=e2190]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2191]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented OS and Kubernetes
                    - link "upgrades" [ref=e2192] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/upgrade-behavior/
                    - text: from starting on Edge clusters when the OS pack values in the cluster profile could not be parsed, for example, because of incorrect YAML indentation. The cluster now reports an
                    - code [ref=e2193]: OSPackValuesValid
                    - text: condition that includes the parsing error and line number.
                - list [ref=e2194]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2195]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented Kubernetes upgrades on Edge clusters that do not use
                    - link "Trusted Boot" [ref=e2196] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/trusted-boot/
                    - text: and were first deployed with Palette agent version 4.8.8 or earlier. The upgrade pods were rejected with the error
                    - code [ref=e2197]: maximum memory usage per Container is 1Gi, but limit is 1536Mi
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2198]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2199]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that could prevent Edge hosts from resetting and returning to registration mode after their
                    - link "cluster was deleted" [ref=e2200] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/remove-clusters/
                    - text: ", if a cleanup step failed during the reset. Affected hosts could not reconnect to Palette."
                - list [ref=e2201]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2202]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - link "certificate check" [ref=e2203] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/certificate-renewal/
                    - text: that runs during Edge cluster creation to restart control plane components, including
                    - code [ref=e2204]: kube-apiserver
                    - text: ", on clusters that use Palette eXtended Kubernetes - Edge (PXK-E). The restart could interrupt add-on packs that were still installing."
                - list [ref=e2205]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2206]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented automatic and manual
                    - link "certificate renewal" [ref=e2207] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/certificate-renewal/
                    - text: on Edge clusters when any aggregated
                    - code [ref=e2208]: APIService
                    - text: in the cluster was unavailable, for example, when
                    - code [ref=e2209]: metrics-server
                    - text: was not ready or an
                    - code [ref=e2210]: APIService
                    - text: remained after uninstalling an add-on such as
                    - code [ref=e2211]: prometheus-adapter
                    - text: . Renewal failed with the error
                    - code [ref=e2212]: unable to retrieve the complete list of server APIs
                    - text: ", and the certificates were not renewed."
                - list [ref=e2213]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2214]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented kube-vip from starting on
                    - link "agent mode" [ref=e2215] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /deployment-modes/agent-mode/
                    - text: Edge hosts that were added to a new cluster after a reset, for example, after their previous cluster was deleted. The control plane VIP of the new cluster was not claimed, and cluster provisioning did not complete.
                - heading "VerteXDirect link to VerteX" [level=3] [ref=e2216]:
                  - text: VerteX
                  - link "Direct link to VerteX" [ref=e2217] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vertex-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e2218]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e2219] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-8"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2220]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2221]:
                    - text: Includes all Palette features, improvements, breaking changes, and deprecations in this release. Refer to the
                    - link "Palette section" [ref=e2222] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: "#palette-enterprise-4.10.a"
                    - text: for more details.
                - heading "Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)Direct link to Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)" [level=3] [ref=e2223]:
                  - text: Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)
                  - link "Direct link to Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)" [ref=e2224] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#virtual-machine-orchestrator-vmo"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "VMO PackDirect link to VMO Pack" [level=4] [ref=e2225]:
                  - text: VMO Pack
                  - link "Direct link to VMO Pack" [ref=e2226] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vmo-pack"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=5] [ref=e2227]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2228] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-15"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2229]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2230]: VM migrations now support VDDK 9 for source VMs on VMware vSphere 7.0 and 8.0. Because Broadcom has removed public VDDK downloads, downloading VDDK requires a Broadcom account with the appropriate product entitlements and an active vSphere subscription.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=5] [ref=e2231]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2232] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-11"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2233]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2234]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the removal of the
                    - link "VMO pack" [ref=e2235] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vmo-pack/
                    - text: to delete the Containerized Data Importer (CDI) even when KubeVirt blocked the removal because virtual machines still existed on the cluster and the uninstall strategy was
                    - code [ref=e2236]: BlockUninstallIfWorkloadsExist
                    - text: . The removal now stops before it deletes any components, so disk imports, cloning, and VM provisioning continue to work.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e2237]:
                  - text: Automation
                  - link "Direct link to Automation" [ref=e2238] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#automation-4"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e2239]:
                  - generic [ref=e2240]:
                    - img [ref=e2242]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e2245]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "Palette CLI" [ref=e2246] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /automation/palette-cli/
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.25 Palette release is 4.10.9. Refer to
                    - link "CLI Tools" [ref=e2247] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/cli-tools/
                    - text: for the download URL and checksum.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e2248]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e2249] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-9"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2250]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2251]:
                    - text: Terraform version 0.30.3 of the
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e2252] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://registry.terraform.io/providers/spectrocloud/spectrocloud/latest/docs
                    - text: is now available. For more details, refer to the Terraform provider
                    - link "release page" [ref=e2253] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/terraform-provider-spectrocloud/releases
                    - text: .
                  - listitem [ref=e2254]:
                    - text: Crossplane version 0.30.3 of the
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e2255] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://marketplace.upbound.io/providers/crossplane-contrib/provider-palette
                    - text: is now available.
                - heading "Docs and EducationDirect link to Docs and Education" [level=3] [ref=e2256]:
                  - text: Docs and Education
                  - link "Direct link to Docs and Education" [ref=e2257] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#docs-and-education"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2258]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2259]:
                    - text: The documented
                    - link "Controllers EKS Policy" [ref=e2260] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/required-iam-policies/additional-iam-policies-specific-use-cases/#controllers-eks-policy
                    - text: now includes the
                    - code [ref=e2261]: iam:ListRoles
                    - text: action, which Palette uses to delete the IRSA roles it manages when you delete an EKS cluster. Refer to the
                    - link "Upgrade Notes" [ref=e2262] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: "#upgrade-notes-4.10.a"
                    - text: for the action required on existing EKS clusters.
                - heading "October 9, 2026 - Component UpdatesDirect link to October 9, 2026 - Component Updates" [level=3] [ref=e2263]:
                  - text: October 9, 2026 - Component Updates
                  - link "Direct link to October 9, 2026 - Component Updates" [ref=e2264] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#component-updates-2026-41"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e2265]: The following components have been updated for Palette version 4.10.25.
                - table [ref=e2266]:
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2267]:
                    - row "Component Version" [ref=e2268]:
                      - columnheader "Component" [ref=e2269]
                      - columnheader "Version" [ref=e2270]
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2271]:
                    - row "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider 0.30.3" [ref=e2272]:
                      - cell "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e2273]:
                        - link "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e2274] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: https://registry.terraform.io/providers/spectrocloud/spectrocloud/latest/docs
                      - cell "0.30.3" [ref=e2275]
                    - row "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider 0.30.3" [ref=e2276]:
                      - cell "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e2277]:
                        - link "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e2278] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: https://marketplace.upbound.io/providers/crossplane-contrib/provider-palette
                      - cell "0.30.3" [ref=e2279]
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e2280]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2281] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-16"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2282]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2283]: Updated Kubernetes core and edge pack distributions across supported minor versions to include pause image enhancements and upgrade constraints.
                - list [ref=e2284]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2285]: Updated Kubernetes pack distributions with the latest maintenance releases and configuration improvements.
                - list [ref=e2286]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2287]:
                    - text: Added support for deploying the
                    - code [ref=e2288]: spectro-mgmt-plane
                    - text: management chart on Amazon EKS 1.36 clusters.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e2289]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2290] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-12"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2291]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2292]: Fixed an issue in MetalLB 0.16.1 Helm packs where liveness and readiness probe ports could not be customized.
                - list [ref=e2293]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2294]: Fixed an issue in Piraeus where RWX (NFS) volumes remained unmountable on single-node clusters when creating multiple RWX persistent volume claims simultaneously.
                - heading "PacksDirect link to Packs" [level=4] [ref=e2295]:
                  - text: Packs
