                - heading "October 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.17-patch.5Direct link to October 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.17-patch.5" [level=2] [ref=e2570]:
                  - text: October 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.17-patch.5
                  - link "Direct link to October 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.17-patch.5" [ref=e2571] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#october-6-2026---release-41017-patch5"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e2572]: Palette 4.10.17-patch.5 is a cumulative security update for Palette 4.10.17. It includes every fix from the earlier 4.10.17 security patches, so upgrading to 4.10.17-patch.5 also applies those fixes. The patch suffix is a build sequence number. It is not a severity rating or a count of fixes.
                - paragraph [ref=e2573]: The following table lists the release date of each patch in this update.
                - table [ref=e2574]:
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2575]:
                    - row "Version Release Date" [ref=e2576]:
                      - columnheader "Version" [ref=e2577]
                      - columnheader "Release Date" [ref=e2578]
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2579]:
                    - row "4.10.17-patch.5 October 6, 2026" [ref=e2580]:
                      - cell "4.10.17-patch.5" [ref=e2581]
                      - cell "October 6, 2026" [ref=e2582]
                    - row "4.10.17-patch.4 October 3, 2026" [ref=e2583]:
                      - cell "4.10.17-patch.4" [ref=e2584]
                      - cell "October 3, 2026" [ref=e2585]
                    - row "4.10.17-patch.2 October 2, 2026" [ref=e2586]:
                      - cell "4.10.17-patch.2" [ref=e2587]
                      - cell "October 2, 2026" [ref=e2588]
                    - row "4.10.17-patch.1 October 1, 2026" [ref=e2589]:
                      - cell "4.10.17-patch.1" [ref=e2590]
                      - cell "October 1, 2026" [ref=e2591]
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=3] [ref=e2592]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e2593] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-4-10-17-patch-5"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2594]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2595]:
                    - text: To improve security, virtual machine operations now also require permissions on the cluster that hosts the virtual machine. Viewing virtual machines and snapshots requires the
                    - code [ref=e2596]: cluster.get
                    - text: permission, and all other operations, such as starting, stopping, cloning, migrating, and creating snapshots, require the
                    - code [ref=e2597]: cluster.update
                    - text: permission (
                    - code [ref=e2598]: virtualCluster.get
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e2599]: virtualCluster.update
                    - text: for virtual clusters). Users assigned the Virtual Machine Power User or Virtual Machine User role together with the Cluster Viewer role can still view virtual machines but can no longer perform other operations on them. Assign these users the Cluster Editor role, or a custom role that includes
                    - code [ref=e2600]: cluster.update
                    - text: ", in the cluster's project. Refer to"
                    - link "VM User Roles and Permissions" [ref=e2601] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/rbac/vm-roles-permissions/
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e2602]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2603] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-17"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2604]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2605]: Applied security fixes to improve the platform's security posture.
                - heading "September 25, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.13Direct link to September 25, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.13" [level=2] [ref=e2606]:
                  - text: September 25, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.13
                  - link "Direct link to September 25, 2026 - PaletteAI VM Launchpad 4.10.13" [ref=e2607] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vm-launchpad-4.10.13"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e2608]: This release delivers version 4.10.13 of the PaletteAI VM Launchpad appliance, which runs on Palette 4.10.17.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=3] [ref=e2609]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e2610] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-10"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2611]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2612]:
                    - text: PaletteAI VM Launchpad now ships as high-availability appliance variants backed by either Piraeus/LINSTOR or Portworx storage. The Piraeus/LINSTOR variant is available in both FIPS and non-FIPS builds; the Portworx variant is available as non-FIPS only. Refer to
                    - link "Install PaletteAI VM Launchpad" [ref=e2613] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/install/
                    - text: and
                    - link "Storage" [ref=e2614] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/infrastructure/storage/
                    - text: for variant selection and storage configuration.
                - list [ref=e2615]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2616]:
                    - text: The appliance UI now includes a
                    - strong [ref=e2617]: Force Stop
                    - text: action for virtual machines. Use
                    - strong [ref=e2618]: Force Stop
                    - text: to immediately power off a virtual machine whose guest operating system does not respond to a graceful
                    - strong [ref=e2619]: Stop
                    - text: . Refer to
                    - link "Manage Virtual Machines" [ref=e2620] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/virtual-machines/managing/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2621]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2622]:
                    - text: PaletteAI VM Launchpad can now forward appliance metrics to Splunk, so you can retain metrics longer and view them in your own tooling. Refer to
                    - link "Metrics and Logs" [ref=e2623] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/metrics-and-logs/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2624]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2625]:
                    - text: The CDI upload proxy and KubeVirt export proxy are now exposed, so
                    - code [ref=e2626]: virtctl image-upload
                    - text: and virtual machine disk exports work through the appliance.
                - list [ref=e2627]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2628]: The appliance now generates the OIDC client secrets for VMO, Headlamp, and the Keycloak admin account per appliance, instead of setting them through hidden profile variables.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e2629]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2630] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-18"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2631]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2632]:
                    - text: The virtual machine resource configuration form now includes a
                    - strong [ref=e2633]: Memory Limits
                    - text: field, corrects a misleading CPU request placeholder, and allows editing CPU and memory requests and limits on day 2.
                - list [ref=e2634]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2635]: The Local UI installation wizard now includes a verify-password field for the VMO password.
                - list [ref=e2636]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2637]:
                    - text: You can now set the Portworx activation ID from the appliance Local UI during Portworx storage setup. Refer to
                    - link "Install PaletteAI VM Launchpad" [ref=e2638] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/install/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2639]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2640]: The ephemeral-storage warning banner can now be dismissed.
                - list [ref=e2641]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2642]: The appliance now bundles Palette VMM version 4.10.x.
                - list [ref=e2643]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2644]: All Helm hook workloads now run as non-root, with the exception of the privileged Multus cleanup job.
                - list [ref=e2645]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2646]:
                    - text: The
                    - code [ref=e2647]: spectro-kubectl
                    - text: images have been rebuilt on Go 1.26.7 to address security vulnerabilities.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e2648]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2649] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-13"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2650]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2651]: Fixed an issue that caused the CDI upload proxy and KubeVirt export proxy ingress to unconditionally render a Traefik configuration, which broke the Nginx reference architecture. This affected VMO pack versions 4.10.2 through 4.10.6.
                - list [ref=e2652]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2653]: Fixed an issue that caused failures when editing a virtual machine's CPU configuration.
                - list [ref=e2654]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2655]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused cloud-init
                    - code [ref=e2656]: networkData
                    - text: to be silently dropped. The appliance now sets
                    - code [ref=e2657]: networkDataSecretRef
                    - text: so the network configuration is applied.
                - list [ref=e2658]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2659]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the virtual machine creation wizard to skip a network interface with
                    - code [ref=e2660]: bootOrder=2
                    - text: when UEFI, Secure Boot, and TPM were enabled.
                - list [ref=e2661]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2662]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented Windows virtual machines using
                    - code [ref=e2663]: windows.*
                    - text: cluster preferences from scheduling on nodes that did not have Hyper-V enlightenment labels.
                - list [ref=e2664]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2665]: Fixed an issue that prevented creating Windows virtual machines from a template.
                - list [ref=e2666]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2667]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented
                    - code [ref=e2668]: Sysprep
                    - text: from running when the QEMU guest agent installation was enabled on Windows.
                - list [ref=e2669]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2670]: Fixed an issue that caused golden images to build non-bootable disks, produce an invalid template, or ignore the EFI boot checkbox.
                - list [ref=e2671]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2672]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the Golden Image Builder to mis-detect the guest operating system from the ISO filename. Refer to
                    - link "Golden Images" [ref=e2673] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/virtual-machines/golden-images/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2674]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2675]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused DataVolumes to display
                    - code [ref=e2676]: ImportScheduled
                    - text: instead of
                    - code [ref=e2677]: ImportInProgress
                    - text: while an import was running.
                - list [ref=e2678]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2679]: Fixed an issue that caused image upload in Direct mode to fail on MAAS and Edge clusters.
                - list [ref=e2680]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2681]: Fixed an issue that caused registry (zot) images to disappear from the UI after a pod restart.
                - list [ref=e2682]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2683]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - strong [ref=e2684]: Storage HA Not Available
                    - text: banner to appear on clusters that do not use LINSTOR.
                - list [ref=e2685]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2686]: Fixed an issue that caused some Traefik routes to be inaccessible.
                - list [ref=e2687]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2688]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused Headlamp SSO to land on a 404
                    - code [ref=e2689]: tenantApps
                    - text: URL after Keycloak login through direct load balancer access.
                - list [ref=e2690]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2691]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the Keycloak configure hook to extract the wrong client ID, which caused a
                    - code [ref=e2692]: CrashLoopBackOff
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2693]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2694]: Fixed an issue that caused the namespace list to include namespaces a user could not access. VMO Manager now scopes the namespace list to the user's Kubernetes token.
                - list [ref=e2695]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2696]: Fixed an issue that prevented cAdvisor metrics from reaching Victoria Metrics, which caused missing dashboard telemetry. This was a regression introduced in 4.9.16.
                - list [ref=e2697]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2698]: Fixed an issue that caused the audit cleanup pod to error out.
                - list [ref=e2699]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2700]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - strong [ref=e2701]: Resource Summary
                    - text: to show usage against allocated resources instead of against cluster capacity.
                - list [ref=e2702]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2703]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - strong [ref=e2704]: Total vCPUs
                    - text: count to be miscalculated. The count now multiplies cores, sockets, and threads.
                - list [ref=e2705]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2706]: Fixed an issue that caused the node CPU history chart to drop to false 0% values.
                - list [ref=e2707]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2708]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the dashboard
                    - strong [ref=e2709]: Issues
                    - text: count to apply an incorrect virtual machine status filter when selected.
                - list [ref=e2710]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2711]: Fixed an issue that prevented dashboard widget changes from persisting in Palette mode.
                - list [ref=e2712]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2713]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused transitional states, such as
                    - code [ref=e2714]: Stopping
                    - text: ", to be counted under"
                    - strong [ref=e2715]: Provisioning
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e2716]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2717]: Fixed an issue that caused edit modals to show a false unsaved-changes prompt when pressing Escape.
                - list [ref=e2718]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2719]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused incorrect breadcrumb labels and a stale
                    - strong [ref=e2720]: System
                    - text: ">"
                    - strong [ref=e2721]: Storage
                    - text: reference.
                - list [ref=e2722]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2723]: Fixed an issue that prevented special characters and uppercase letters from pasting correctly in the VNC console.
                - heading "September 24, 2026 - Component UpdatesDirect link to September 24, 2026 - Component Updates" [level=2] [ref=e2724]:
                  - text: September 24, 2026 - Component Updates
                  - link "Direct link to September 24, 2026 - Component Updates" [ref=e2725] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#component-updates-2026-39"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e2726]: The following components have been updated for Palette version 4.10.16 - 4.10.17.

                    - row "Component Version" [ref=e2729]:
                      - columnheader "Component" [ref=e2730]
                      - columnheader "Version" [ref=e2731]
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2732]:
                    - row "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider 0.30.2" [ref=e2733]:
                      - cell "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e2734]:

                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e2741]:

                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2743]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2744]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue in the Spectro Cloud Terraform provider where concurrent resource reconciliation could leak the
                    - code [ref=e2745]: ProjectUid
                    - text: header across tenant and project scopes, leading to permission errors when managing mixed-scope resources.

                - heading "September 18, 2026 - Component UpdatesDirect link to September 18, 2026 - Component Updates" [level=2] [ref=e2853]:
                  - text: September 18, 2026 - Component Updates
                  - link "Direct link to September 18, 2026 - Component Updates" [ref=e2854] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#component-updates-2026-38"
                    - text: "#"
                - paragraph [ref=e2855]: The following components have been updated for Palette versions 4.10.16 - 4.10.17 and 4.9.51 - 4.10.17.
                - table [ref=e2856]:
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2857]:
                    - row "Component Version" [ref=e2858]:
                      - columnheader "Component" [ref=e2859]
                      - columnheader "Version" [ref=e2860]
                  - rowgroup [ref=e2861]:
                    - row "Artifact Studio 4.10.4" [ref=e2862]:
                      - cell "Artifact Studio" [ref=e2863]:
                        - link "Artifact Studio" [ref=e2864] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /downloads/artifact-studio/
                      - cell "4.10.4" [ref=e2865]
                    - row "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider 0.30.1" [ref=e2866]:

                    - row "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider 0.30.1" [ref=e2870]:
                      - cell "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e2871]:
                        - link "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e2872] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: https://marketplace.upbound.io/providers/crossplane-contrib/provider-palette
                      - cell "0.30.1" [ref=e2873]

                      - cell "Palette Management Appliance" [ref=e2875]:
                        - link "Palette Management Appliance" [ref=e2876] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /enterprise-version/install-palette/palette-management-appliance/
                      - cell "4.10.17" [ref=e2877]
                    - row "VerteX Management Appliance 4.10.17" [ref=e2878]:
                      - cell "VerteX Management Appliance" [ref=e2879]:
                        - link "VerteX Management Appliance" [ref=e2880] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /vertex/install-palette-vertex/vertex-management-appliance/
                      - cell "4.10.17" [ref=e2881]
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e2882]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2883] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-19"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2884]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2885]: Palette now blocks access to production SaaS, Artifact Studio, and product downloads from comprehensively sanctioned jurisdictions at the network edge, to meet export control compliance requirements.
                - heading "PacksDirect link to Packs" [level=3] [ref=e2886]:
                  - text: Packs
                  - link "Direct link to Packs" [ref=e2887] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#packs-7"
                    - text: "#"
                - table [ref=e2888]:

                      - columnheader "Pack Name" [ref=e2891]

                - heading "September 17, 2026 - Release 4.10.17Direct link to September 17, 2026 - Release 4.10.17" [level=2] [ref=e2985]:
                  - text: September 17, 2026 - Release 4.10.17

                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e2987]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e2988] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-20"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2989]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2990]:
                    - text: Added FIPS provider images to the content bundle when building content with the
                    - code [ref=e2991]: "--fips"
                    - text: flag in Palette CLI.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e2992]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e2993] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-15"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e2994]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2995]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where mixed compression formats in
                    - code [ref=e2996]: spectro-ui
                    - text: container image manifests caused unpack failures in containerd.
                - list [ref=e2997]:
                  - listitem [ref=e2998]: Fixed an issue where airgapped Enterprise Cluster installations failed to pull cert-manager images because the generated manifest referenced the upstream registry and an unpublished image tag instead of the local registry.
                - list [ref=e2999]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3000]: Fixed an issue that caused the Palette CLI shell completion command to fail.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e3001]:

                    - text: The

                      - /url: /automation/palette-cli/
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.17 Palette release is 4.10.4. Refer to
                    - link "CLI Tools" [ref=e3011] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/cli-tools/
                    - text: for the download URL and checksum.
                - heading "September 11, 2026 - Component UpdatesDirect link to September 11, 2026 - Component Updates" [level=2] [ref=e3012]:

                    - text: "#"

                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=3] [ref=e3037]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e3038] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-21"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3039]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3040]: Added support for deploying the NVIDIA GPU operator in airgapped Edge clusters using provider images with pre-installed NVIDIA drivers.
                - list [ref=e3041]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3042]: Deprecated Portworx pack versions 3.3 and older to align with upstream vendor support lifecycle policies.
                - list [ref=e3043]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3044]:
                    - text: Made
                    - code [ref=e3045]: admin_kube_config
                    - text: reads during cluster
                    - code [ref=e3046]: Read()
                    - text: operations conditional, gracefully handling 403 forbidden responses when users lack download permissions.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e3047]:

                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3049]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3050]: Fixed an issue in the Harbor pack where Palette-managed projects were created as public without a configuration setting to set them to private.

                  - listitem [ref=e3052]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where running the Palette CLI shell completion command failed if the user had not first executed
                    - code [ref=e3053]: palette login
                    - text: .
