                - heading "September 10, 2026 - Release 4.10.16Direct link to September 10, 2026 - Release 4.10.16" [level=2] [ref=e3153]:
                  - text: September 10, 2026 - Release 4.10.16
                  - link "Direct link to September 10, 2026 - Release 4.10.16" [ref=e3154] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#september-10-2026---release-41016"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e3155]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3156] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-17"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3157]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3158]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where
                    - code [ref=e3159]: stylus-operator
                    - text: did not retry the registry connection when the
                    - code [ref=e3160]: zot-secret
                    - text: appeared late, which left pack images missing from Zot.
                - list [ref=e3161]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3162]: Fixed an issue where Edge Kubernetes upgrades to version 1.33.5 stalled following a hostname-validation reboot due to x509 certificate verification failures during Stylus pack download and edge login.
                - list [ref=e3163]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3164]: Fixed an issue on two-node Edge clusters where PostgreSQL failed to start after a provider image swap, because the TLS key pair paths were split across persistent and ephemeral storage.
                - list [ref=e3165]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3166]: Fixed an issue where Private Cloud Gateway (PCG) migrations did not transfer IP pool ownership.
                - list [ref=e3167]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3168]: Fixed an issue where Ally upgrades retriggered on-boot operating system patching drains across nodes, causing nodes with failed drains to remain cordoned indefinitely.
                - list [ref=e3169]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3170]: Fixed an issue during vSphere Private Cloud Gateway (PCG) migrations where the cloud account's overlord UID was not transferred, preventing migrated clusters from being deleted.
                - list [ref=e3171]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3172]: Fixed an issue where two-node cluster creation deadlocked permanently when the API VIP was unavailable due to nodes mutually fencing each other and evicting kube-vip.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e3173]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e3174] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-6"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e3175]:
                  - generic [ref=e3176]:
                    - img [ref=e3178]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e3181]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e3182] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.16 Palette release is 4.10.4.
                - heading "September 8, 2026 - Release 4.10.14Direct link to September 8, 2026 - Release 4.10.14" [level=2] [ref=e3183]:
                  - text: September 8, 2026 - Release 4.10.14
                  - link "Direct link to September 8, 2026 - Release 4.10.14" [ref=e3184] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#september-8-2026---release-41014"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=3] [ref=e3185]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3186] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-18"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3187]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3188]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where some OIDC sign-ins failed with an
                    - code [ref=e3189]: OidcRedirectUriNotAllowed
                    - text: error. This affected
                    - code [ref=e3190]: kubectl oidc-login
                    - text: authentication from headless hosts and access to the Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO) console.
                - heading "September 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.13Direct link to September 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.13" [level=2] [ref=e3191]:
                  - text: September 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.13
                  - link "Direct link to September 6, 2026 - Release 4.10.13" [ref=e3192] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#release-notes-4.10.0"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Security NoticesDirect link to Security Notices" [level=3] [ref=e3193]:
                  - text: Security Notices
                  - link "Direct link to Security Notices" [ref=e3194] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#security-notices-2"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3195]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3196]:
                    - text: Review the
                    - link "Security Bulletins" [ref=e3197] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /security-bulletins/reports/
                    - text: page for the latest security advisories.
                - heading "Palette EnterpriseDirect link to Palette Enterprise" [level=3] [ref=e3198]:
                  - text: Palette Enterprise
                  - link "Direct link to Palette Enterprise" [ref=e3199] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#palette-enterprise-4.10.0"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=4] [ref=e3200]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e3201] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-4.10.0"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3202]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3203]:
                    - text: Palette now validates user-supplied cluster profile and app profile versions against the
                    - link "Semantic Versioning" [ref=e3204] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://semver.org
                    - text: specification when a profile is created or updated through the Palette UI, API, Terraform provider, or Crossplane provider. Values such as
                    - code [ref=e3205]: 1.2.3
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e3206]: 1.2.3-rc.1
                    - text: are accepted; values such as
                    - code [ref=e3207]: 2.2.2.develop
                    - text: or
                    - code [ref=e3208]: V0.0.1
                    - text: that earlier releases accepted are now rejected whenever a version is set (on create, on clone, when creating a new profile version, or when changing the version of an existing profile). Existing profiles carrying a malformed version continue to function, and no pre-upgrade or post-upgrade action is required. If you update a profile's version to a valid value, later attempts to set a malformed value on that profile fail, including reverting to the original value. These new requirements do not apply to external registry and chart tags, including Zarf UDS tags. For the accepted format, refer to
                    - link "Version a Cluster Profile" [ref=e3209] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /profiles/cluster-profiles/modify-cluster-profiles/version-cluster-profile/
                    - text: and
                    - link "Version an App Profile" [ref=e3210] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /profiles/app-profiles/modify-app-profiles/version-app-profile/
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e3211]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3212]:
                    - text: The OCI Helm registry validation endpoint now returns an error code that reflects the actual failure. Previously, any validation failure (including request timeouts, DNS failures, and TLS errors) returned
                    - code [ref=e3213]: InvalidRegistryCredentials
                    - text: . Timeout and cancellation failures now return a timeout error, connectivity failures return an unreachable error, and
                    - code [ref=e3214]: InvalidRegistryCredentials
                    - text: is reserved for authentication failures. Existing registries and credentials are unaffected, and no pre-upgrade or post-upgrade action is required. If you have automation that treats
                    - code [ref=e3215]: InvalidRegistryCredentials
                    - text: as the catch-all validation failure, narrow it to authentication failures and handle timeout and connectivity errors with a retry instead. Refer to
                    - link "Registries" [ref=e3216] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3217]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3218]:
                    - text: Palette removes the deprecated
                    - code [ref=e3219]: GET /v1/projects
                    - text: endpoint. Use
                    - code [ref=e3220]: POST /v1/dashboard/projects
                    - text: instead, which supports the filtering and sorting that the removed endpoint lacked. Update any automation, script, or integration that calls
                    - code [ref=e3221]: GET /v1/projects
                    - text: before you upgrade, because those calls fail after the upgrade.
                - list [ref=e3222]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3223]:
                    - text: Creating or updating a registry now requires a non-empty
                    - code [ref=e3224]: metadata.name
                    - text: . Palette validates the field at the API layer and rejects a missing or empty name with an HTTP 400 response that identifies
                    - code [ref=e3225]: metadata.name
                    - text: . Earlier releases performed no field-level check, so you could create a registry with an empty name, and only the uniqueness constraint on the tenant and name pair rejected it later, which surfaced as a misleading duplicate-name error. The validation applies to pack, Helm, basic OCI, and ECR OCI registries. Existing registries are unaffected, and no pre-upgrade or post-upgrade action is required. Update any automation or Terraform configuration that templates a registry payload and might pass an unset name. Refer to
                    - link "Registries" [ref=e3226] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3227]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3228]:
                    - text: Updating an OCI registry that uses the Helm or Zarf provider no longer accepts a change to the synchronization setting. Palette treats synchronization as a create-only setting for these registries, and the Palette UI does not offer the toggle after creation. The update API accepted the change and returned success without applying it. The API now rejects the request with an HTTP 400 response and the message
                    - code [ref=e3229]: Registry synchronization setting cannot be changed after the registry is created
                    - text: . This applies to both basic and ECR OCI registries. If you manage registries with the Terraform provider, remove any change to
                    - code [ref=e3230]: is_synchronization
                    - text: on an existing
                    - code [ref=e3231]: spectrocloud_registry_oci
                    - text: resource. Such a change previously produced persistent
                    - code [ref=e3232]: terraform plan
                    - text: drift, because each apply reported success without taking effect. Refer to
                    - link "Registries" [ref=e3233] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /registries-and-packs/registries/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3234]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3235]:
                    - text: The Edge host search API,
                    - code [ref=e3236]: POST /v1/dashboard/edgehosts/search
                    - text: ", now has its own sort field contract. The accepted sort fields are"
                    - code [ref=e3237]: name
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e3238]: state
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e3239]: healthState
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e3240]: creationTimestamp
                    - text: ", and"
                    - code [ref=e3241]: lastModifiedTimestamp
                    - text: . Palette removes the
                    - code [ref=e3242]: environment
                    - text: ","
                    - code [ref=e3243]: clusterName
                    - text: ", and"
                    - code [ref=e3244]: clusterState
                    - text: fields, which do not apply to Edge hosts. A request that sorts on one of the removed fields now returns an HTTP 422 response instead of an HTTP 500 response. Sorting on
                    - code [ref=e3245]: healthState
                    - text: was advertised in earlier releases but did not return results, and now works. Remove the unsupported fields from any automation or integration that sorts Edge host search results.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e3246]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e3247] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-11"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3248]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3249]:
                    - text: Apache CloudStack support is now generally available. Palette removes the
                    - strong [ref=e3250]: ApacheCloudStack
                    - text: feature flag and enables Apache CloudStack for all tenants. You can deploy and manage Kubernetes clusters in your Apache CloudStack environment through a Private Cloud Gateway (PCG). Refer to
                    - link "Apache CloudStack" [ref=e3251] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/data-center/cloudstack/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3252]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3253]:
                    - text: Canonical Kubernetes (CK8s) clusters on MAAS now support SSH key management. You can configure
                    - strong [ref=e3254]: SSH Keys
                    - text: on the cluster's cloud configuration during cluster creation and on Day-2 through the Palette UI, API, Terraform provider, and Crossplane provider, on both Palette and Palette VerteX. Palette injects the keys into the
                    - code [ref=e3255]: spectro
                    - text: user's
                    - code [ref=e3256]: ~/.ssh/authorized_keys
                    - text: on every control plane and worker node, and preserves any users that MAAS or the machine image already configured. Changing the keys on a deployed CK8s cluster repaves the cluster nodes. Refer to
                    - link "SSH Keys on MAAS Cluster Nodes" [ref=e3257] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/data-center/maas/architecture/#ssh-keys-on-maas-cluster-nodes
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3258]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3259]:
                    - text: You can now use dynamic credentials when backing up edge-native, AKS, vSphere, and other non-AWS clusters to AWS S3, which allows you to authenticate backups without storing long-lived static keys. Previously, only AWS IaaS and EKS clusters supported dynamic credentials. Refer to
                    - link "Add Backup Location using Dynamic Credentials" [ref=e3260] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/backup-restore/add-backup-location-dynamic/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3261]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3262]:
                    - text: Canonical Kubernetes (CK8s) clusters on MAAS now support Network Time Protocol (NTP) server configuration. You can configure
                    - strong [ref=e3263]: NTP Servers
                    - text: on the cluster's cloud configuration during cluster creation and on Day-2 through the Palette UI, API, Terraform provider, and Crossplane provider, on both Palette and Palette VerteX. The servers you specify replace the NTP configuration that MAAS provides to each control plane and worker node. Changing the servers on a deployed CK8s cluster repaves the cluster nodes. Refer to
                    - link "NTP Servers on MAAS Cluster Nodes" [ref=e3264] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/data-center/maas/architecture/#ntp-servers-on-maas-cluster-nodes
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3265]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3266]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3267]:
                      - text: Palette now supports the option to skip worker node upgrades on
                      - link "Azure IaaS" [ref=e3268] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/azure/create-azure-cluster/
                      - text: ","
                      - link "GCP IaaS" [ref=e3269] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/gcp/create-gcp-iaas-cluster/
                      - text: ", and"
                      - link "Apache CloudStack" [ref=e3270] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/data-center/cloudstack/create-manage-cloudstack-clusters/
                      - text: clusters. For example, if you have worker pools running critical databases or real-time processing services, you can enable this option to maintain service continuity during control plane upgrades, then schedule
                      - link "worker node updates" [ref=e3271] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cluster-updates/#trigger-worker-node-upgrade
                      - text: during planned maintenance windows.
                    - paragraph [ref=e3272]:
                      - text: The version difference between the control plane and worker nodes must not exceed the
                      - link "N-3 minor version skew supported by Kubernetes" [ref=e3273] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: https://kubernetes.io/releases/version-skew-policy/
                      - text: . Palette enforces this during cluster profile updates and blocks you from updating if you attempt to exceed the N-3 threshold.
                - list [ref=e3274]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3275]:
                    - text: You can now view whether the image pull secret that is specified during install has propagated properly to all clusters. Clusters cannot pull security-hardened images from Spectro Cloud's OCI registries until they have the pull secret. Refer to
                    - link "Monitor Propagation of the Image Pull Secret" [ref=e3276] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /enterprise-version/system-management/configure-image-pull-secret/#monitor-propagation
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e3277]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e3278] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-22"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3279]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3280]:
                    - text: The
                    - strong [ref=e3281]: Host LXD-based control planes
                    - text: and
                    - strong [ref=e3282]: Use LXD VMs
                    - text: toggles in cluster and node configuration now display a tooltip clarifying that LXD-based VMs on MAAS are supported only with Palette eXtended Kubernetes clusters. Enabling LXD with other Kubernetes distributions results in deployment failures.
                - list [ref=e3283]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3284]: Headlamp, the modern replacement for the deprecated Kubernetes Dashboard, is now available on imported clusters as well as Palette-managed clusters.
                - list [ref=e3285]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3286]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3287]:
                      - text: Palette now updates IAM roles in AWS when the
                      - code [ref=e3288]: irsaRoles
                      - text: parameter of the EKS pack is updated. Previously, specified IAM roles were created in AWS, but not updated when the
                      - code [ref=e3289]: irsaRoles
                      - text: field changed. Edits to
                      - code [ref=e3290]: irsaRoles
                      - text: in the cluster profile take effect on the next reconcile. For example, policies are attached and detached, service account changes update the trust policy, and removing an entry deletes the underlying IAM role. When you delete an EKS cluster, Palette deletes every IRSA role that it manages for that cluster.
                    - paragraph [ref=e3291]:
                      - text: If you have existing EKS clusters that use the
                      - code [ref=e3292]: irsaRoles
                      - text: field, you should add the
                      - code [ref=e3293]: iam:ListRolePolicies
                      - text: action to the
                      - strong [ref=e3294]: PaletteControllersEKSPolicy
                      - text: AWS IAM policy so that Palette can fully manage the lifecycle of the roles.
                - list [ref=e3295]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3296]: The cert-manager chart deployed with the Palette management plane has been upgraded from version 1.14 to version 1.20.2.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e3297]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3298] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-19"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3299]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3300]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where using the Palette UI to open and save a
                    - link "cluster profile" [ref=e3301] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /profiles/cluster-profiles/
                    - text: created with the API or Terraform could reorder its packs, surfacing as unexpected
                    - code [ref=e3302]: terraform plan
                    - text: drift for profiles managed as code.
                - list [ref=e3303]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3304]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - code [ref=e3305]: import-presetup-logs
                    - text: ConfigMap to grow without limit each time the setup job for
                    - link "importing a cluster" [ref=e3306] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/imported-clusters/cluster-import/
                    - text: ran. The ConfigMap eventually exceeded the maximum request size, causing
                    - code [ref=e3307]: request is too large
                    - text: errors and leaving the management plane API server unresponsive.
                - list [ref=e3308]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3309]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - code [ref=e3310]: palette-webhook
                    - text: deployment to stall with a pod stuck in
                    - code [ref=e3311]: Pending
                    - text: and the deployment reporting
                    - code [ref=e3312]: ProgressDeadlineExceeded
                    - text: "on"
                    - link "Amazon EKS clusters" [ref=e3313] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: with three or fewer nodes. The stall occurred both when a Palette agent upgrade updated the webhook and after a cluster pivot.
                - list [ref=e3314]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3315]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the CoreDNS deployment in the
                    - code [ref=e3316]: kube-system
                    - text: namespace to be replaced with a virtual cluster CoreDNS configuration on
                    - link "MAAS clusters" [ref=e3317] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/data-center/maas/
                    - text: that host virtual clusters. Cluster DNS resolution failed and the cluster reported an unknown status in Palette.
                - list [ref=e3318]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3319]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented the Palette UI from updating when all tags were removed from a
                    - link "cluster profile" [ref=e3320] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /profiles/cluster-profiles/
                    - text: . The removed tags continued to display until the browser was refreshed.
                - list [ref=e3321]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3322]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused intermittent
                    - code [ref=e3323]: 504 Gateway Timeout
                    - text: responses when attaching
                    - link "cluster profiles" [ref=e3324] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /profiles/cluster-profiles/
                    - text: to a cluster through the
                    - code [ref=e3325]: "PUT /v1/spectroclusters/{uid}/profiles"
                    - text: API while that cluster was actively reporting its status. Attaching several profiles to the same cluster in quick succession was the most common trigger.
                - list [ref=e3326]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3327]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused multi-line error messages to appear truncated in a cluster's
                    - strong [ref=e3328]: Events
                    - text: tab, so an event showed only
                    - code [ref=e3329]: "\"Reconciler error\" err=<"
                    - text: without the failure description that followed it. The complete message is now recorded as a single event. Only error-level events were affected.
                - list [ref=e3330]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3331]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused changing a cluster-level tag on a healthy
                    - link "Amazon EKS cluster" [ref=e3332] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/public-cloud/aws/eks/
                    - text: to emit a
                    - code [ref=e3333]: ClusterUpgradeTriggered
                    - text: event and replace every node in the cluster's worker node pools. Pipelines that update tag values on each run, such as compliance tagging, could therefore repave nodes repeatedly. Changing tags directly on a worker node pool still replaces that pool's nodes.
                - list [ref=e3334]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3335]: Fixed an issue that caused sustained high CPU usage by the Palette management plane. The management plane repeatedly reconciled packs whose configuration had not changed, consuming approximately 1.3 vCPU per affected cluster continuously. Cluster provisioning and pack functionality were not affected.
                - list [ref=e3336]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3337]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue where
                    - link "OS patching" [ref=e3338] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/os-patching/
                    - text: on Ubuntu cluster nodes could leave a node in
                    - code [ref=e3339]: Ready,SchedulingDisabled
                    - text: and unable to accept workloads, and could prevent scheduled patches from running. Palette now requires OS patch schedules to run no more frequently than hourly.
                - list [ref=e3340]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3341]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the
                    - link "cert-manager" [ref=e3342] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/cluster-management/cert-manager-addon/
                    - text: add-on pack to fail with
                    - code [ref=e3343]: ChartInstallFailed
                    - text: when the pack enabled Gateway API support before the Gateway API custom resource definitions existed on the cluster. Palette now applies the setting once those definitions are available.
                - heading "Deprecations and RemovalsDirect link to Deprecations and Removals" [level=4] [ref=e3344]:
                  - text: Deprecations and Removals
                  - link "Direct link to Deprecations and Removals" [ref=e3345] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#deprecations-and-removals-2"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3346]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3347]:
                    - text: K3s is deprecated as a Palette Virtual Cluster distribution and will be removed in a future Palette release. K3s was deprecated in Palette 4.8.0, when the cluster group distribution selector in the Palette UI began marking K3s as deprecated and moved the Kubernetes distribution to the top of the list. Existing virtual clusters that use K3s continue to run. Use the Kubernetes distribution when you create new cluster groups and virtual clusters. Refer to
                    - link "Palette Virtual Clusters" [ref=e3348] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/palette-virtual-clusters/
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "EdgeDirect link to Edge" [level=3] [ref=e3349]:
                  - text: Edge
                  - link "Direct link to Edge" [ref=e3350] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#edge-7"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e3351]:
                  - generic [ref=e3352]:
                    - img [ref=e3354]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e3357]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "CanvOS" [ref=e3358] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/CanvOS
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.13 Palette release is 4.10.3.
                - heading "Breaking ChangesDirect link to Breaking Changes" [level=4] [ref=e3359]:
                  - text: Breaking Changes
                  - link "Direct link to Breaking Changes" [ref=e3360] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#breaking-changes-edge-4.10.0"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3361]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3362]:
                    - text: The Palette agent now applies a fixed 45-minute time limit to each attempt to download and extract the provider image on an Edge host. The agent does not resume partial downloads, so each new attempt starts from the beginning. On connections with limited bandwidth, attempts can repeatedly reach the limit, which leaves the cluster in the
                    - strong [ref=e3363]: Provisioning
                    - text: state or prevents the agent upgrade from completing. If your Edge hosts have limited bandwidth, make the provider image available on the host instead of downloading it. For the symptoms and steps, refer to the
                    - link "provider image download times out" [ref=e3364] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---provider-image-download-times-out-on-slow-network-connections
                    - text: troubleshooting scenario.
                - heading "Upgrade NotesDirect link to Upgrade Notes" [level=4] [ref=e3365]:
                  - text: Upgrade Notes
                  - link "Direct link to Upgrade Notes" [ref=e3366] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#upgrade-notes-edge-4.10.0"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3367]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3368]:
                    - text: Upgrade the
                    - link "Longhorn CSI" [ref=e3369] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /integrations/packs/?pack=csi-longhorn
                    - text: pack to
                    - code [ref=e3370]: 1.12.0
                    - text: if your Edge clusters run Linux kernel 6.19 or later, including Hadron
                    - code [ref=e3371]: 7.1.3
                    - text: and Ubuntu
                    - code [ref=e3372]: 7.0.0-generic
                    - text: . Because of a
                    - link "known issue" [ref=e3373] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /release-notes/known-issues/
                    - text: on these kernels, shared Read-Write-Many (RWX) volumes served by Longhorn CSI versions
                    - code [ref=e3374]: 1.8.x
                    - text: through
                    - code [ref=e3375]: 1.11.2
                    - text: ", or by the Piraeus RWX driver, can fail to open with a remote I/O error. For the affected combinations, symptoms, and a node-level workaround, refer to the"
                    - link "shared volumes fail on kernel 7.x" [ref=e3376] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /troubleshooting/edge/#scenario---shared-volumes-fail-on-kernel-7x-due-to-nfsv41-directory-delegations
                    - text: troubleshooting scenario.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e3377]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e3378] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-12"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3379]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3380]:
                    - text: Locally managed Edge clusters in an airgapped environment that use Palette eXtended Kubernetes Edge (PXK-E) or Canonical Kubernetes now support decoupled control plane and worker node upgrades. Enable the
                    - strong [ref=e3381]: Skip worker node update (Optional)
                    - text: toggle on a worker pool in Local UI to hold that pool at its current Kubernetes version while the control plane advances, up to the Kubernetes N-3 minor version skew. This reduces how many times worker nodes repave when crossing several Kubernetes minor versions. Scale-up on a pool with the toggle enabled is rejected, and disabling the toggle repaves the pool to the control plane version. Refer to
                    - link "Decoupled Control Plane and Worker Node Upgrades" [ref=e3382] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/upgrade-behavior/#decoupled-control-plane-and-worker-node-upgrades
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3383]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3384]:
                    - text: Airgap content bundle uploads to Edge hosts are now chunked, resumable, and parallel by default. The Palette CLI splits the bundle into chunks and transfers them over multiple connections, which shortens upload times for large bundles on links with a high bandwidth-delay product, and verifies each chunk with SHA-256. An interrupted upload resumes from the chunks the Edge host already holds instead of restarting. You can also stream a bundle straight from a signed object store URL with the new
                    - code [ref=e3385]: "--src-url"
                    - text: flag rather than staging a local copy. The earlier single-stream upload remains available with
                    - code [ref=e3386]: "--legacy"
                    - text: ", and the CLI falls back to it automatically against Edge hosts that predate chunked upload support. Refer to"
                    - link "Upload Content Bundle" [ref=e3387] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/cluster-management/upload-content-bundle/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3388]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3389]:
                    - text: Edge clusters in appliance mode can now use systemd extensions to deliver Kubernetes and Palette Agent binaries at runtime, instead of embedding those binaries in the provider image. This feature is a Technical Preview. This applies in both connected and airgapped environments. On operating systems running systemd version 255 or later, provider images built with CanvOS 4.10.x exclude the binaries by default, and Stylus (Palette Edge node agent) 4.10.x delivers them through systemd extensions. Set
                    - code [ref=e3390]: "system.uri: NA"
                    - text: in the BYOOS pack for standard upgrades. Unified Kernel Image (UKI) deployments do not support systemd extensions and continue to receive these binaries embedded in the provider image. Refer to
                    - link "Deliver Kubernetes and Agent Binaries via systemd Extensions" [ref=e3391] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edgeforge-workflow/palette-canvos/build-provider-images/systemd-extensions/
                    - text: for build and upgrade guidance.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e3392]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e3393] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-23"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3394]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3395]: The Custom UI appliance install wizard now renders a specific inline error at any field whose value is invalid. Sections that contain an invalid field display an "invalid" badge on the section title, and the step counter reads as "X of Y complete."
                - list [ref=e3396]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3397]:
                    - text: Edge workflows have been updated to Kairos v4.1.2 with
                    - code [ref=e3398]: kairos-init
                    - text: v0.16.x. Day-1 and Day-2 upgrades from earlier Kairos builds are supported.
                - list [ref=e3399]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3400]:
                    - text: The Palette TUI landing page now signposts initial user setup. When no login user exists on the Edge host, the landing page displays the yellow warning
                    - strong [ref=e3401]: "Setup required: press F2 to create login user for ssh and LocalUI"
                    - text: ", and the footer reads"
                    - strong [ref=e3402]:
                      - code [ref=e3403]: <F2> Create login
                    - text: instead of
                    - strong [ref=e3404]:
                      - code [ref=e3405]: <F2> Customize
                    - text: . Both revert automatically once a login user is created. Previously, the landing page did not indicate that a user account was missing, so the F2 shortcut for creating the initial login was not discoverable. Refer to
                    - link "Initial Edge Host Configuration with Palette TUI" [ref=e3406] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/site-installation/initial-setup/
                    - text: for more information.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e3407]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3408] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-20"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3409]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3410]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented digest-pinned application images from being redirected to the
                    - link "local registry" [ref=e3411] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/deploy-custom-registries/local-registry/
                    - text: on airgapped Edge clusters. Pods that referenced an image by digest rather than by tag attempted to pull from the upstream registry and remained in
                    - code [ref=e3412]: ImagePullBackOff
                    - text: .
                - list [ref=e3413]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3414]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused packs whose name contains a forward slash, such as Helm OCI packs sourced from a private registry, to fail to download on Edge hosts with
                    - code [ref=e3415]: "failed to rename pack: no such file or directory"
                    - text: errors.
                - list [ref=e3416]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3417]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused NTP servers entered in the
                    - link "Palette TUI" [ref=e3418] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/site-installation/initial-setup/
                    - text: as a comma-separated list without a space, such as
                    - code [ref=e3419]: 10.10.180.0,10.10.180.1
                    - text: ", to generate an invalid time synchronization configuration. Both comma-separated and comma-and-space-separated lists are now accepted."
                - list [ref=e3420]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3421]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented
                    - link "remote shell" [ref=e3422] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/cluster-management/remote-shell/
                    - text: access to an Edge host from being automatically disabled after 24 hours of inactivity. If the service handling remote shell sessions restarted unexpectedly, the Edge host remained enabled for remote shell indefinitely, leaving the tunnel and its temporary user credentials active.
                - list [ref=e3423]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3424]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented the NTP servers configured on an Edge host through the
                    - link "Palette TUI" [ref=e3425] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/site-deployment/site-installation/initial-setup/
                    - text: from being visible in Local UI. The Edge host overview page now lists them in an
                    - strong [ref=e3426]: NTP Servers
                    - text: field.
                - list [ref=e3427]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3428]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that left a
                    - link "two-node Edge cluster" [ref=e3429] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/architecture/two-node/
                    - text: without a Kubernetes API server after the loss of the leader node. The surviving node did not complete its promotion, so the cluster virtual IP served no traffic. Loss of the follower node was unaffected.
                - list [ref=e3430]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3431]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that could place the Kubernetes datastore of a
                    - link "two-node Edge cluster" [ref=e3432] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/architecture/two-node/
                    - text: on ephemeral rather than persistent storage. Data written since the last persistent snapshot was lost on reboot, and the node could not be promoted during failover.
                - list [ref=e3433]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3434]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that prevented leader promotion from completing on a connected-mode
                    - link "two-node Edge cluster" [ref=e3435] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/architecture/two-node/
                    - text: while Palette was unreachable, leaving the local control plane unavailable until connectivity was restored. Promotion now completes without Palette connectivity.
                - heading "VerteXDirect link to VerteX" [level=3] [ref=e3436]:
                  - text: VerteX
                  - link "Direct link to VerteX" [ref=e3437] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vertex-2"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e3438]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e3439] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-13"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3440]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3441]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3442]: FIPS-compiled vCluster is now available, letting strict-FIPS tenants provision virtual clusters using FIPS 140-3 approved cryptography. The FIPS-compiled pack is automatically selected when deploying virtual clusters on VerteX.
                  - listitem [ref=e3443]:
                    - paragraph [ref=e3444]:
                      - text: Includes all Palette features, improvements, breaking changes, and deprecations in this release. Refer to the
                      - link "Palette section" [ref=e3445] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: "#palette-enterprise-4.10.0"
                      - text: for more details.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e3446]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e3447] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-24"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3448]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3449]: Palette VerteX now invalidates all active JWTs for a session when a user logs out or changes their password. Previously, tokens remained valid after logout and could be reused.
                - heading "Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)Direct link to Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)" [level=3] [ref=e3450]:
                  - text: Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)
                  - link "Direct link to Virtual Machine Orchestrator (VMO)" [ref=e3451] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#virtual-machine-orchestrator-vmo-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "VMO PackDirect link to VMO Pack" [level=4] [ref=e3452]:
                  - text: VMO Pack
                  - link "Direct link to VMO Pack" [ref=e3453] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#vmo-pack-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=5] [ref=e3454]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3455] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-21"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3456]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3457]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused the CA certificate help text in the
                    - strong [ref=e3458]: Certificate validation
                    - text: step of the
                    - link "Create Provider" [ref=e3459] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-migration-assistant/create-source-providers/
                    - text: wizard to refer to the OpenShift API endpoint for every provider type, including VMware vSphere.
                - list [ref=e3460]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3461]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused uploading a
                    - link "golden image" [ref=e3462] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/virtual-machines/golden-images/
                    - text: to fail with a
                    - strong [ref=e3463]: Certificate Trust Required
                    - text: prompt that contained no URL, leaving no way to accept the certificate and complete the upload. This occurred when the upload was proxied through Palette because the cluster had no direct upload URL available. The upload pages now display a notice before an upload begins, and a proxied upload that fails states that direct upload access has to be enabled on the VMO pack.
                - list [ref=e3464]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3465]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue that caused hotplug disk attachments to stall on Portworx-backed clusters. The
                    - code [ref=e3466]: DataVolume
                    - text: for the hotplug disk previously remained in
                    - code [ref=e3467]: ImportScheduled
                    - text: when other virtual machines in the same cluster used only container disks. Hotplug attachment now proceeds correctly in this scenario.
                - list [ref=e3468]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3469]:
                    - text: Clarified access control for the
                    - strong [ref=e3470]: KubeVirt Configuration
                    - text: page. The page is available only to users who hold cluster-admin privileges and who authenticate through OIDC. Refer to
                    - link "KubeVirt Configuration" [ref=e3471] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/kubevirt-configuration/
                    - text: and
                    - link "VMO Roles" [ref=e3472] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /vm-management/vm-launchpad/access-management/vmo-roles/
                    - text: for more information.
                - list [ref=e3473]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3474]: Fixed an issue that prevented a namespace created through the console from appearing in the UI until the page was reloaded. New namespaces now appear immediately after they are created.
                - heading "AutomationDirect link to Automation" [level=3] [ref=e3475]:
                  - text: Automation
                  - link "Direct link to Automation" [ref=e3476] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#automation-6"
                    - text: "#"
                - generic [ref=e3477]:
                  - generic [ref=e3478]:
                    - img [ref=e3480]
                    - text: info
                  - paragraph [ref=e3483]:
                    - text: The
                    - link "Palette CLI" [ref=e3484] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /automation/palette-cli/
                    - text: version corresponding to the 4.10.13 Palette release is 4.10.3. Refer to
                    - link "CLI Tools" [ref=e3485] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /downloads/cli-tools/
                    - text: for the download URL and checksum.
                - heading "FeaturesDirect link to Features" [level=4] [ref=e3486]:
                  - text: Features
                  - link "Direct link to Features" [ref=e3487] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#features-14"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3488]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3489]:
                    - text: Terraform version 0.30.0 of the
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Terraform provider" [ref=e3490] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://registry.terraform.io/providers/spectrocloud/spectrocloud/latest/docs
                    - text: is now available. For more details, refer to the Terraform provider
                    - link "release page" [ref=e3491] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/terraform-provider-spectrocloud/releases
                    - text: .
                  - listitem [ref=e3492]:
                    - text: Crossplane version 0.30.0 of the
                    - link "Spectro Cloud Crossplane provider" [ref=e3493] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://marketplace.upbound.io/providers/crossplane-contrib/provider-palette
                    - text: is now available.
                - heading "ImprovementsDirect link to Improvements" [level=4] [ref=e3494]:
                  - text: Improvements
                  - link "Direct link to Improvements" [ref=e3495] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-25"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3496]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3497]: Palette CLI is now available for ARM Linux (arm64) and macOS Apple Silicon (arm64).
                - list [ref=e3498]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3499]: The Palette CLI now confirms content bundle uploads immediately. Previously, after the upload progress bar reached 100%, the CLI could stay silent for several minutes while the Edge host unpacked the bundle. The CLI now reports upload completion as soon as the transfer finishes.
                - list [ref=e3500]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3501]:
                    - text: Added the
                    - code [ref=e3502]: is_synchronization
                    - text: attribute to the
                    - code [ref=e3503]: spectrocloud_registry_helm
                    - text: Terraform resource to enable synchronization controls and align functionality with OCI registries.
                - list [ref=e3504]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3505]: Added support for configuring registry synchronization settings and credential options in the Terraform provider.
                - list [ref=e3506]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3507]: Enhanced Terraform provider registry resource handling and configuration options.
                - heading "Bug FixesDirect link to Bug Fixes" [level=4] [ref=e3508]:
                  - text: Bug Fixes
                  - link "Direct link to Bug Fixes" [ref=e3509] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#bug-fixes-22"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3510]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3511]:
                    - text: Fixed an issue in
                    - code [ref=e3512]: spectrocloud_registry_helm
                    - text: where missing TLS configuration options prevented TLS authentication from being sent, causing Helm registries to be stored with TLS disabled.
                - heading "Docs and EducationDirect link to Docs and Education" [level=3] [ref=e3513]:
                  - text: Docs and Education
                  - link "Direct link to Docs and Education" [ref=e3514] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#docs-and-education-1"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3515]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3516]:
                    - text: Documentation has been added explaining that Local UI, the Palette TUI, and the Palette API all change Edge host passwords as root, and that PAM exempts root from password quality checks unless the image sets the
                    - code [ref=e3517]: enforce_for_root
                    - text: option. Without it, the check still logs a
                    - code [ref=e3518]: BAD PASSWORD
                    - text: message but returns success, so a password that does not meet your policy is accepted. Refer to
                    - link "Change User Password" [ref=e3519] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/local-ui/host-management/access-console/#change-user-password
                    - text: for the ways a password can be changed, and
                    - link "Build Edge Artifacts" [ref=e3520] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /clusters/edge/edgeforge-workflow/palette-canvos/
                    - text: for SUSE, Ubuntu, and RHEL examples that set the option at image build time.
                - heading "PacksDirect link to Packs" [level=3] [ref=e3521]:
                  - text: Packs
                  - link "Direct link to Packs" [ref=e3522] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#packs-9"
                    - text: "#"

                - heading "Pack NotesDirect link to Pack Notes" [level=4] [ref=e3660]:
                  - text: Pack Notes
                  - link "Direct link to Pack Notes" [ref=e3661] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#pack-notes"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3662]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3663]:
                    - text: The
                    - code [ref=e3664]: tigera-operator
                    - text: 3.32.1 pack has been republished as
                    - code [ref=e3665]: tigera-operator-3.32.1-rev1
                    - text: to add the
                    - code [ref=e3666]: calico/csi
                    - text: and
                    - code [ref=e3667]: calico/node-driver-registrar
                    - text: images to its image manifest. The Tigera Operator deploys these two images whenever
                    - code [ref=e3668]: kubeletVolumePluginPath
                    - text: is set to a value other than
                    - code [ref=e3669]: None
                    - text: ", but because they were missing from the manifest they were never mirrored into dedicated or airgapped registries, leaving the"
                    - code [ref=e3670]: csi-node-driver
                    - text: DaemonSet in
                    - code [ref=e3671]: ImagePullBackOff
                    - text: . Calico networking was unaffected, because the Container Storage Interface (CSI) driver is a separate optional component.
                - list [ref=e3672]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3673]: Fixed an issue where the Spectro RBAC add-on pack preset included live example namespaces that were automatically deployed when no namespaces were specified in the profile layer.
                - heading "Deprecations and RemovalsDirect link to Deprecations and Removals" [level=4] [ref=e3674]:
                  - text: Deprecations and Removals
                  - link "Direct link to Deprecations and Removals" [ref=e3675] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#deprecations-and-removals-3"
                    - text: "#"
                - list [ref=e3676]:
                  - listitem [ref=e3677]:
                    - text: The Ingress Nginx pack is disabled in Palette 4.10.13. You can no longer create new cluster profiles that include the pack. Existing profiles that already contain it continue to launch new clusters, and running workloads are unaffected. The pack was deprecated in Palette 4.8.0, following the Kubernetes project's
                    - link "retirement of ingress-nginx" [ref=e3678] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: https://kubernetes.io/blog/2025/11/11/ingress-nginx-retirement/
                    - text: . The pack remains disabled for approximately three months before it transitions to the Deleted state, after which existing profiles can no longer launch new clusters.
              - generic [ref=e3679]:
                - generic [ref=e3681]:
                  - text: "Tags:"
                  - list [ref=e3682]:
                    - listitem [ref=e3683]:
                      - link "release-notes" [ref=e3684] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /tags/release-notes/
                - link "Edit this page" [ref=e3687] [cursor=pointer]:
                  - /url: https://github.com/spectrocloud/librarium/blob/master/docs/docs-content/release-notes/release-notes.md
                  - img [ref=e3688]
                  - text: Edit this page
            - navigation "Docs pages" [ref=e3692]:
              - link "Next Announcements »" [ref=e3693] [cursor=pointer]:
                - /url: /release-notes/announcements/
                - generic [ref=e3694]: Next
                - generic [ref=e3695]: Announcements »

              - link "November 15, 2026 - Release 4.10.44" [ref=e3700] [cursor=pointer]:

              - link "November 6, 2026 - Component Updates" [ref=e3722] [cursor=pointer]:

              - list [ref=e3723]:
                - listitem [ref=e3724]:
                  - link "Improvements" [ref=e3725] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: "#improvements-3"
                - listitem [ref=e3726]:
                  - link "Bug Fixes" [ref=e3727] [cursor=pointer]:

                - listitem [ref=e3728]:
                  - link "Packs" [ref=e3729] [cursor=pointer]:

  1  | import * as fs from "fs";
  2  | import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";
  3  | import { extractSitemapPathnames, WaitForDocusaurusHydration } from "./utils";
  4  | import excludeList from "./exclude.json";
  5  | 
  6  | const siteUrl = "http://localhost:3000";
  7  | const sitemapPath = "build/sitemap.xml";
  8  | const stylesheetPath = "visuals/screenshot.css";
  9  | const stylesheet = fs.readFileSync(stylesheetPath).toString();
  10 | 
  11 | test.describe.configure({ mode: "parallel" });
  12 | 
  13 | function isVersionedDocsPathname(pathname: string, excludeList: string[]): boolean {
  14 |   if (
  15 |     excludeList.some((excludedPath) => {
  16 |       if (excludedPath.endsWith("/*")) {
  17 |         // Let's remove the trailing "/*" to match sub-paths
  18 |         const basePath = excludedPath.slice(0, -2);
  19 |         // Exclude sub-paths only, not the index page
  20 |         return pathname.startsWith(basePath) && pathname !== `${basePath}/`;
  21 |       }
  22 |       // This is an exact match
  23 |       return pathname === excludedPath;
  24 |     })
  25 |   ) {
  26 |     return false;
  27 |   }
  28 | 
  29 |   // Additional exclusion criteria
  30 |   if (pathname.startsWith("/api/") || pathname.match(/\/\d+\.\d+\.x\//)) {
  31 |     return false;
  32 |   }
  33 | 
  34 |   return true;
  35 | }
  36 | 
  37 | function screenshotPathname(pathname: string) {
  38 |   test(`pathname ${pathname}`, async ({ page }) => {
  39 |     console.log(`Taking screenshot of ${pathname}`);
  40 |     const url = siteUrl + pathname;
  41 |     await page.goto(url);
  42 |     await page.waitForFunction(WaitForDocusaurusHydration);
  43 |     await page.waitForLoadState("domcontentloaded");
  44 |     await page.addStyleTag({ content: stylesheet });
  45 |     await page.waitForTimeout(1000); // Waits for 1000 milliseconds
> 46 |     await expect(page).toHaveScreenshot({ fullPage: true, timeout: 10000 });
     |                        ^ Error: expect(page).toHaveScreenshot(expected) failed
  47 |   });
  48 | }
  49 | 
  50 | test.describe("Docs screenshots", () => {
  51 |   const pathnames = extractSitemapPathnames(sitemapPath).filter((pathname) =>
  52 |     isVersionedDocsPathname(pathname, excludeList)
  53 |   );
  54 | 
  55 |   pathnames.forEach(screenshotPathname);
  56 | });
